  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的端到端机器人导航框架存在不可预测的黑箱问题，而指令遵循方法又无法实现完全自主，因此需要一种既能利用VLM的高级社会推理能力，又能保证可解释性和安全性的导航框架。

Method: G2-Nav不直接让VLM做出规划决策，而是将其语义推理转化为具有可靠性和可解释性的视觉语言成本图；VLM对可通行区域和社会主体进行开放集感知并将社会上下文映射到成本图中；通过语义验证上游跟踪结果，并引入高频安全检查来防范系统延迟对轨迹生成的影响。

Result: 在真实世界的实验中，G2-Nav在非结构化环境中实现了安全、高效且符合社会规范的自主导航。

Conclusion: G2-Nav通过将VLM的社会推理转化为可解释的成本图，并结合语义验证与安全检查，有效弥合了抽象社会推理与现实安全部署之间的鸿沟，为社交导航提供了既智能又可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Social navigation requires the robot to reason and respond in complex real-world environments. While recent works attempt to incorporate human-level intelligence into robot planning using large Vision-Language Models (VLMs), end-to-end frameworks often create an unpredictable black-box, and existing instruction-following methods are not designed for full autonomy. To bridge this gap, we present G2-Nav, a novel framework that grounds abstract social reasoning and guards safe real-world deployment. Instead of asking the VLM for direct planning decisions, G2-Nav translates its semantic reasoning into a vision-language costmap with reliability and interpretability. The VLM evaluates traversable regions and social agents from open-set perception, mapping social context into the costmap. To improve real-world robustness, the VLM performs semantic verification on upstream tracking, and we introduce a high-frequency safety check to guard against system latency prior to trajectory generation. We demonstrate through real-world experiments that G2-Nav delivers safe, efficient, and socially compliant autonomous navigation in unstructured environments. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [63] [User-Driven Learning from Demonstration: A Trajectory and Impedance Learning Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16998)
*Zi-Qi Yang,Mehrdad R. Kermani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用户驱动的机器人示教学习方法，通过集成3D快速微分同胚匹配算法、动态系统运动生成器、扩展卡尔曼滤波和阻抗参数化函数，实现单次示教下的实时学习与高精度复现，同时确保柔顺性并降低用户负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于时间索引的轨迹缺乏柔顺性，在人机交互中存在安全隐患；且重复示教同一任务效率低下，用户负担重。因此需要一种既能保证柔顺性与精度、又能减少用户示教次数的方法。

Method: 将3D快速微分同胚匹配算法与动态系统运动生成器结合实现单次示教学习与复现；利用扩展卡尔曼滤波补偿复现误差并恢复外部干扰；引入阻抗参数化函数从示教中学习阻抗变化，维持特定任务下的表面接触。

Result: 在7自由度KUKA LWR IV+机器人上进行了综合实验验证，结果表明该方法能够实现高精度复现、实时学习阻抗变化，并有效应对外部扰动，提升人机交互安全性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效降低了用户示教负担，实现了单次示教下的柔顺、精确且具有鲁棒性的机器人技能学习与复现，适用于需要接触力控制的交互任务场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a method for user-driven robot Learning from Demonstration (LfD) that reduces user effort while ensuring compliant and precise reproduction. The method eliminates repeated teaching for the same task and enables real-time learning from a single demonstration. Demonstrated motions are reproduced with high precision, while impedance variations are learned in real time to provide both compliance and robustness against perturbations. This mitigates potential safety issues in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) that arise from conventional time-indexed trajectories lacking compliance. The proposed approach integrates a three-dimensional (3D) Fast Diffeomorphic Matching (FDM) algorithm with a Dynamical System (DS)-based motion generator to achieve real-time single-shot demonstration learning and reproduction. An Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) framework compensates for reproduction errors and recovers from external interactions. Furthermore, an impedance parameterization function is incorporated to learn impedance variations from demonstrations and maintain surface contact for specific applications. The proposed approach is validated through comprehensive experiments on a 7 Degree-of-Freedom (DOF) KUKA LWR IV+ robot.

</details>


### [64] [ADMM-Based Safety-Critical Distributed NMPC for Cooperative Transportation by Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17007)
*Ruturaj S. Sambhus,Kapi Ketan Mehta,Yicheng Zeng,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向四足机器人团队协同搬运的安全关键分布式非线性模型预测控制框架，通过ADMM分解实现分布式实时优化，并利用高阶控制障碍函数保障安全避障，仿真和实验验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四足机器人团队协同搬运共享载荷时面临的安全关键控制问题，包括动态耦合约束、实时分布式优化、安全避障以及对通信延迟和不确定性的鲁棒性需求。

Method: 采用分布式非线性模型预测控制框架，将集中式最优控制问题通过ADMM分解为并行的局部NMPC子问题，实现载荷状态和交互力轨迹的共识，并引入高阶控制障碍函数强化安全避障约束。

Result: 与集中式NMPC相比，平均NLP求解时间降低23%，同时保持相当的闭环性能；消融实验表明载荷状态共识和完整约束显著提升载荷跟踪与分布式协调能力。

Conclusion: 所提分布式框架能有效实现四足机器人团队在复杂环境下的安全协同搬运，对载荷不确定性和外部扰动具有鲁棒性，在计算效率上优于集中式方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a safety-critical distributed nonlinear model predictive control (DNMPC) framework for cooperative payload transportation by teams of quadrupedal robots. The proposed approach models the robotic team and the shared payload as a dynamically coupled networked system with rigid holonomic coupling constraints arising from cooperative transportation. To enable distributed real-time optimization, the centralized finite-horizon optimal control problem is decomposed into parallel local NMPC subproblems coordinated through the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). The resulting distributed framework enforces consensus over both payload-state and interaction-wrench trajectories while explicitly incorporating acceleration-level holonomic coupling constraints within the distributed predictive control formulation. Safety-critical obstacle avoidance constraints for both the robotic agents and payload are enforced using higher-order control barrier functions (HOCBFs). The framework is validated through numerical simulations with teams of two, three, and four quadrupedal robots transporting shared payloads in cluttered environments. Real-time experiments on two- and three-robot teams demonstrate safe and robust transportation under payload uncertainty and external disturbances. Compared with centralized NMPC, the proposed framework achieves up to 23% reduction in average NLP solve time while maintaining comparable closed-loop performance. Ablation studies further demonstrate robustness to communication delays and show that explicit payload-state consensus and holonomic constraints substantially improve payload tracking and distributed coordination over existing wrench-only consensus formulations.

</details>


### [65] [Articulated Humanoid Head for a Robot Receptionist Capable of Natural Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17042)
*Tharusha Fonseka,Charuka Bandara,Moshintha Hewavitharana,Melisa Arukgoda,Wageesha N. Manamperi,Udaya S. K. Perera Miriya Thanthrige,Peshala Jayasekara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款面向接待员角色的类人机器人头部，具有21个自由度、硅胶皮肤和集成化架构，结合人脸识别（SCRFD、ArcFace、ByTetrack）与自然语言处理（Llama、Whisper）实现实时交互与人再识别，用户研究显示类人程度平均得分4.13/5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人机器人头部存在成本高、机械复杂、环境适应性有限等问题，难以满足社交交互、教育和服务等场景对自然高效人机交互的需求。

Method: 设计了一个具有21个自由度的机械结构（包含嘴、眼、眉、颈部），覆盖仿真硅胶皮肤；集成基于模型的架构，使用SCRFD+ArcFace+ByTetrack进行人脸识别，Llama+Whisper进行自然语言处理，硬件支持实时操作和人再识别。

Result: 定量测量了对话能力和人再识别性能；通过用户研究评估情感表达力和类人程度，类人程度平均得分为4.13/5。

Conclusion: 所设计的类人机器人头部在类人外观、情感表达和实时交互方面表现良好，有望应用于接待等社交场景。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have become increasingly popular in applications such as social interaction, education, and service roles, which drives the need for more natural and efficient human-robot interactions. However, currently available humanoid heads often face limitations, including high costs, mechanical complexity, and limited adaptability across diverse environments. To address these challenges, we present an articulated humanoid robot head designed for a receptionist role, integrating a mechanical structure with 21 degrees of freedom (DoF), including mechanisms for the mouth, eyes, eyebrows, and neck, and covered with realistic silicone skin to achieve a human-like appearance and expression. The system integrates a model-based architecture that combines SCRFD, ArcFace, and ByTetrack for face recognition and Llama and Whisper for natural language processing, with hardware support enabling real-time operations and human re-identification. The conversational ability and re-identification capabilities of the humanoid robot head were quantitatively measured, while its emotional expressiveness and human likeness were evaluated through a user study, achieving an average human likeness score of 4.13 out of 5.

</details>


### [66] [SHAPE: Simultaneous Water Hydraulic Actuation and Position Estimation of a Sensorless Remote Actuator through a Thin and Long Flexible Tube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17054)
*Yuki Nakamura,Shuto Yoshimura,Tomoyuki Noda,Yoshihiro Nakata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用充水柔性软管实现传感器缺失工况下远程动力传输与位置估计的方法，通过在50米长管内建立压力波动与体积损失的模型，实现了无传感器水压缸的稳定位置控制，并通过现场参数辨识适应软管及气体混入的变化，从而提升机器人在恶劣环境下的远程可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恶劣环境中，机器人传感器和电子设备容易失效；水液压驱动虽可通过细长软管实现无传感器远程操作并降低关节阻抗，但由于低阻抗和有限可视性，在负载变化下难以精确定位关节/末端执行器。

Method: 提出基于充水柔性软管的方法，通过对传输过程中基于压力波动的体积损失建模并考虑少量空气混入，实现在长达50米软管中间步传输驱动力与位置估计；建立无传感器反馈控制框架，并开发现场参数辨识方法以适应软管和空气混入的变异性。

Result: 实验验证表明，该方法能在负载变化下实现无传感器水液压缸的稳定位置控制，且现场参数辨识方法可有效处理软管与空气混入的变异性。

Conclusion: 该研究促进了机器人在恶劣环境中的可靠远程控制，解决了此前无传感器系统中难以实现的反馈控制问题。

Abstract: Robot sensors and electronic equipment are prone to failure in harsh environments. With water hydraulic drive, thin and long tubes enable remote operation without actuator-side sensors. Furthermore, the elasticity of the tubes reduces the impedance of the joints (actuators), benefiting robot tasks involving unexpected contact with the environment or vibrations. However, owing to the low impedance and limited camera visibility, accurately positioning the joint (or end effector) to the target location under varying load conditions is challenging. This study proposes a novel method that employs water-filled flexible tubes to enable the transmission of driving power and actuator-side information to and from the actuator, respectively, without actuator-side sensors. By modeling volumetric loss during transmission based on pressure fluctuations and incorporating minor air entrapment, simultaneous power transmission and position estimation is achieved through a tube up to 50 m. Thus, it becomes possible to use a feedback control framework that was previously difficult to implement in sensorless systems. Experimental validation confirms stable position control of a sensorless water hydraulic cylinder under varying loads. Furthermore, a field parameter-identification method accounts for tube and air entrainment variability without requiring actuator-side sensors. These contributions promote reliable remote control of robots in harsh environments.

</details>


### [67] [DROID-ANCHOR: Odometry-Anchored Recurrent Metric Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17058)
*Yuxuan Chen,Brook Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Metric-DROID提出一种端到端递归架构，通过集成本体感知里程计将视觉SLAM锚定到物理现实，采用LSTM更新算子、不确定性感知度量后端($BA_{odom}$)和选择性残差微调策略，有效解决单目系统的尺度模糊和漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视觉SLAM系统固有无尺度歧义和尺度漂移问题，现有基于递归光流的SLAM方法虽鲁棒性优异但仍存在尺度模糊，亟需将视觉SLAM锚定到物理真实尺度。

Method: (1) LSTM更新算子：将高频里程计序列编码为空间特征图，提供持久度量偏差用于迭代优化；(2) 不确定性感知度量后端($BA_{odom}$)：将里程计视为几何锚点并学习异方差协方差，通过回归时变度量不确定性$\Sigma_o$智能平衡视觉重投影与度量平移残差；(3) 选择性残差微调策略：保留预训练几何先验的同时实现零样本度量对齐。

Result: 该方法有效缓解了轮子打滑和传感器噪声的影响，实现了零样本的度量对齐。

Conclusion: 通过集成本体感知里程计及其不确定性建模，成功将递归光流SLAM从尺度模糊提升为度量精确的SLAM系统。

Abstract: Precise metric depth estimation is fundamental for autonomous robot navigation, yet monocular systems inherently suffer from scale ambiguity and scale drift. While recent recurrent flow-based SLAM systems have demonstrated state-of-the-art robustness, they remain scale-ambiguous. In this paper, we propose Metric-DROID, an end-to-end recurrent architecture that anchors visual SLAM to physical reality by integrating proprioceptive odometry. Our framework introduces the following innovations: (1) A LSTM Update Operator that encodes high-frequency odometry sequences into spatial feature maps, providing a persistent metric bias for iterative refinement. (2) An Uncertainty-Aware Metric Backend ($BA_{odom}$) that treats odometry as a geometric anchor with learned heteroscedastic covariance. By regressing a time-varying metric uncertainty $Σ_{o}$, our system intelligently balances visual re-projection and metric translation residuals, effectively mitigating the impact of wheel-slip and sensor noise. (3) We further propose a selective residual fine-tuning strategy to preserve pre-trained geometric priors while enabling zero-shot metric alignment.

</details>


### [68] [Move First, Commit Later: Selective LiDAR-to-BIM Global Initialization via Sequential Consensus with Symmetry-Aware Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17103)
*Yujie Zhang,Yuxuan Guo,Jiashuo Zheng,Zeheng Fan,Xiaohui Jia,Jinyue Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"先移动，后提交"（Move First, Commit Later）层，解决LiDAR-to-BIM初始化中的对称混淆问题。该层将配准前端作为证据源，通过跨子图共识、拓扑门控和类型化决策（提交/延迟/模糊），实现可逆的对称感知定位，在测试中正确提交所有轨迹并避免误匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无先验位姿的LiDAR-to-BIM全局初始化中，重复性室内环境的主要失败模式不是低置信度配准，而是自信的混叠（confident aliasing）：子图可以匹配多个BIM区域并获得相近分数，产生由对称性导致的位移伪位姿。现有方法缺乏对这类对称混淆的检测与处理机制。

Method: 提出Move First, Commit Later选择性层：1) 将配准前端视为证据源，多个子图的候选位姿映射到公共SE(2)锚点；2) top-1共识B_m聚合跨子图证据，对非冠军多样性不变；3) 拓扑仅作为二元可行性门控。决策类型化为COMMIT、DEFER或AMBIGUOUS(τ)（报告检测到的对称周期），且可逆——打破对称的运动可将AMBIGUOUS升级为COMMIT。

Result: 在真实多房间建筑和受控对称仿真中，该层在所有测试的轨迹-尺度配置下均正确提交，否则以类型化状态弃权；而同一前端上的强制选择策略在大多数情况下选择了错误的房间。提交的锚点位置在0.02-0.36 m内（相对独立激光追踪器参考），方向误差在1.9°以内（相对BIM配准方向代理）。

Conclusion: 所提出的层能够在对称环境下可靠地判断何时提交定位结果、何时因混淆而延迟，并通过类型化状态提供对称周期信息以引导后续运动。该层设计为前端模块化，虽仅在一栋建筑和一个前端上验证，但展示了解决全局LiDAR-to-BIM初始化中对称混叠问题的有效范式。

Abstract: Global LiDAR-to-BIM initialization must place a robot within an as-designed building model without a prior pose. In repetitive interiors, the principal failure mode is not low-confidence registration but confident aliasing: a submap can match several BIM regions with comparable scores, producing a high-scoring pose displaced by symmetry. We present Move First, Commit Later, a selective layer that treats a registration front-end as an evidence source and decides whether to commit. Candidates from multiple submaps are mapped to a common $\mathrm{SE}(2)$ anchor; a top-1 consensus $B_m$, invariant to non-champion multiplicity, aggregates cross-submap evidence; and topology serves only as a binary feasibility gate. The decision is typed--COMMIT, DEFER, or AMBIGUOUS($τ$), reporting the detected symmetry period--and reversible: symmetry-breaking motion upgrades AMBIGUOUS to COMMIT. On a real multi-room building and a controlled symmetric simulation, the layer commits correctly in every tested trajectory-scale configuration and otherwise abstains with a typed state, whereas forced-choice policies on the same front-end select wrong rooms in most cases. Committed anchors are within 0.02--0.36 m of an independent laser-tracker position reference and within $1.9^\circ$ of a BIM-registration orientation proxy. The evaluation covers one building and one front-end; the layer is designed to be front-end modular.

</details>


### [69] [BoxTwin: Learning Elastoplastic Articulated Object Dynamics from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17132)
*Heng Zhang,Gehan Zheng,Kaifeng Zhang,Jay Song,Shivansh Patel,Sonny Hu,Yunzhu Li,Changxi Zheng,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BoxTwin是一个交互式数字孪生框架，能够从视频中学习弹塑性铰接物体的完整动力学，包括非线性弹性、塑性屈服和损伤累积。实验证明该框架能够准确跟踪关节轨迹并在长时间范围内再现接触后塑性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生模型难以处理弹塑性铰接物体(EAOs)的非线性弹性、塑性屈服和损伤累积等复杂物理行为，需要一种能从视频中学习并预测这些动态特性的框架。

Method: BoxTwin流程包括：从视频重建场景，为每个EAO识别物理感知的本构模型，结合视频驱动重建与弹塑性损伤建模，实现对EAO完整动力学的学习。

Result: 在手动折叠和双臂操作EAOs的实验上，BoxTwin能够准确跟踪关节轨迹，并在长时间范围内可靠再现接触后塑性行为。

Conclusion: BoxTwin通过将视频驱动重建与弹塑性损伤建模相结合，推动数字孪生向非结构化环境中可变形铰接物体的预测性自适应控制发展。

Abstract: Digital twins enable robots to anticipate and adapt to physical interactions, but existing models struggle with elastoplastic articulated objects (EAOs) that exhibit nonlinear elasticity, plastic yielding, and damage accumulation. We present BoxTwin, an interactive digital twin framework that learns the full dynamics of EAOs from videos. Our pipeline reconstructs the scene, identifies a physics aware constitutive model for each EAO. Experiments on manual folding and dual arm manipulation of EAOs show that BoxTwin accurately tracks joint trajectories and reproduces post contact plastic behavior over long horizons. By integrating video driven reconstruction with elastoplastic damage modeling, BoxTwin advances digital twins toward predictive, adaptive control of deformable articulated objects in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [70] [VIDAR: Visual-Inertial Dense Alignment and Reconstruction via a Geometric Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17171)
*Diyari Mohammed Salih,Lingxiang Hu,Naima AitOufroukh-Mammar,Fabien Bonardi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VIDAR是一种将SVO+IMU里程计与Depth Anything 3结合的视觉-惯性密集重建框架，通过视觉-惯性前端提供度量锚点（相机位姿、尺度和世界坐标系），融合基础模型的局部几何实现全局重建。在EuRoC上位姿注入将尺度误差降低至约1%，F@0.10达0.463；解耦混合策略无需真值位姿将F@0.10提升至0.676。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目基础模型能提供密集几何信息但缺乏稳定的度量尺度，需要引入视觉-惯性信息来解决尺度模糊问题，实现度量级密集单目重建。

Method: 将SVO+IMU里程计作为前端提供度量锚点（位姿、尺度和一致世界坐标系），结合Depth Anything 3的密集几何预测，通过位姿条件注入或解耦对齐策略融合局部几何到全局重建中。

Result: 在EuRoC上，位姿注入策略尺度误差降至约1%，F@0.10达0.463；解耦混合策略在无真值位姿情况下将F@0.10提升至0.676。

Conclusion: VIDAR提供了一种实用的度量级密集单目重建路线，有效地结合了视觉-惯性里程计的度量精度与基础模型的密集几何能力。

Abstract: Monocular foundation models provide dense geometry but usually lack a stable metric scale. This paper presents VIDAR, a visual-inertial dense reconstruction framework that couples SVO+IMU odometry with Depth Anything 3. VIDAR uses the visual-inertial front end as a metric anchor: it provides camera poses, scale, and a consistent world frame for aligning dense foundation-model predictions across time. The foundation model then contributes detailed local geometry that is fused into a global reconstruction. We study both pose-conditioned DA3 and a decoupled alignment strategy. On EuRoC, pose injection reduces scale error to about 1\% and reaches 0.463 mean F@0.10; the decoupled hybrid improves this to 0.676 without ground-truth poses. Results on EuRoC and TUM RGB-D show that VIDAR is a practical route to metric dense monocular reconstruction.

</details>


### [71] [Retriever: Composing Closed-Loop Asynchronous Robot Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17213)
*Linfeng Zhao,Haojie Huang,Jiayuan Mao,Weiyu Liu,Mykel Kochenderfer,Lawson L. S. Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Retriever 提出了一种跨全栈的异步决策系统框架，通过有状态因果流函数图、显式运行时钟和确定性回放机制，解决了长时域机器人代理系统中并发时序不确定性和调度依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时域机器人系统采用临时拼凑的并发和发布/订阅约定，导致时序和输入消费语义隐式化，产生难以复现、调试和重用的调度依赖行为。现有方案仅在算法层或系统层解决部分问题，缺乏跨全栈的统一方案。

Method: Retriever 包含异步决策模型、编程模型、运行时和闭环代理管道。将代理表示为在显式运行时钟上执行的有状态因果流函数图，通过连续时间流上的异步环境-代理循环形式化，证明有限记忆因果策略可由这些算子组合表示，并编译为支持多后端运行时的系统。

Result: 通过真实机器人案例研究、运行时开销受控实验和确定性回放行为实验进行评估。

Conclusion: Retriever 通过跨全栈的异步建模和系统设计，有效解决了机器人闭环系统中因隐式时序语义导致的可复现性和调试难题，兼顾了系统效率和确定性行为。

Abstract: Building long-horizon robot agents requires composing closed-loop pipelines -- perception, belief update, planning, and control -- whose components run at different clocks and with variable latency. Today, these systems are often assembled with ad-hoc concurrency and pub/sub conventions that make timing and input-consumption semantics implicit, yielding schedule-dependent behavior that is hard to reproduce, debug, and reuse. Current solutions typically solve parts of this problem at either the algorithmic or the systems layer, but not both. In this work, we propose Retriever, which spans the entire stack: an asynchronous decision model, a programming model, a runtime, and an example closed-loop agent pipeline. Retriever represents an agent as a graph of stateful causal stream functions executed on explicit run clocks. We formalize this view via an asynchronous environment-agent loop over continuous-time streams and show that finite-memory causal policies can be represented by compositions of these operators. Retriever compiles these graphs into a runtime that supports multiple backends, enabling systematic debugging across running environments and deterministic replay from logged asynchronous data. We evaluate Retriever through a real-robot case study together with controlled studies of runtime overhead and deterministic replay behavior.

</details>


### [72] [Asynchronous Multimodal Diffusion Policy Composition via Latency-Aware Guidance Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17257)
*Zihao He,Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Cewu Lu,Haoshu Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LAG-Fusion框架，通过延迟感知的引导融合实现异步多模态扩散策略组合，在接触式操作中融合低频视觉策略和高频力觉策略，解决了不同模态传感速率和推理延迟不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态扩散策略通常依赖同步融合或人工设计的多频率架构，导致高频反馈变慢或缺乏对新模态组合的可扩展性，需要一种能处理不同模态在信息内容、传感速率和推理延迟方面差异的异步融合框架。

Method: 提出LAG-Fusion框架，允许各模态策略以自身原生推理速率运行并在可用时提供去噪引导；推导出相对动作表示下扩散变量的参考帧重基规则，使延迟的引导在融合前对齐；在接触式操作中实例化低频视觉+高频力觉策略组合。

Result: 在异构模态延迟实验下，LAG-Fusion相比同步融合和专门设计的力感知基线，提升了策略响应速度和任务性能。

Conclusion: LAG-Fusion通过延迟感知的异步引导融合有效解决了多模态扩散策略中传感速率和推理延迟异构的问题，为机器人模仿学习中的多模态融合提供了更具扩展性和响应性的方案。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have shown strong potential for robotic imitation learning, and recent extensions incorporate additional modalities to improve manipulation performance. However, these modalities often differ not only in information content but also in sensing rates and inference latencies. Existing multimodal diffusion policies typically rely on synchronous fusion or manually designed multi-frequency architectures, which either slow down high-frequency feedback or limit extensibility to new modality combinations. We propose LAG-Fusion, a latency-aware guidance fusion framework for asynchronous multimodal diffusion policy composition. LAG-Fusion allows modality-specific policies to operate at their native inference rates and contribute denoising guidance whenever available. To make asynchronous composition consistent, we derive a reference-frame rebasing rule for diffusion variables under relative action representations, enabling delayed guidance to be aligned before fusion. We instantiate LAG-Fusion in contact-rich manipulation by composing a low-frequency vision policy with a high-frequency force policy. Experiments under heterogeneous modality latencies show that LAG-Fusion improves policy responsiveness and task performance over synchronous fusion and specially designed force-aware baselines.

</details>


### [73] [From Perception to Assistance: Open-Vocabulary Shared Autonomy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17323)
*Murilo Vinicius da Silva,Ricardo V. Godoy,Juliano Negri,Gustavo J. G. Lahr,Ranulfo Bezerra,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种共享自主框架，通过RGB-D相机捕捉操作员动作、VLM定位目标、视频分割跟踪、GPU加速MPC控制器避障及势场引导，在四足移动机械臂上实现了高精度遥操作，在工业任务中成功率100%，且碰撞与辅助模块缺一不可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机界面难以满足工业环境下的高精度遥操作需求，特别是在有限深度感知、杂乱环境中精确对齐末端执行器与目标并避免碰撞的挑战。

Method: 1) 单RGB-D相机无穿戴设备捕捉操作员手臂运动与手势；2) 视觉-语言模型(VLM)通过自由文本提示定位目标；3) 可提示视频分割模型跨相机跟踪目标，生成与障碍物地图分离的抓取框架；4) GPU加速模型预测控制器(MPC)执行运动指令，基于在线体积重建实现自我与环境碰撞避免；5) 势场在最终接近阶段校正操作员参考朝向目标；6) 手势触发自主模式完成抓取。

Result: 位置RMSE为59mm（相对于动作捕捉真值）；控制器在操作员故意将机械臂向障碍物推进6cm时仍保持与障碍物至少18cm距离；工业阀门操作和拾放任务中完整框架全部成功，消融碰撞或辅助模块均产生失败，自主模式在每项5次试验中成功4次。

Conclusion: 所提出的共享自主框架能有效结合操作员意图与自主避障及目标引导能力，在工业遥操作任务中达到高成功率和安全性，碰撞避免与辅助模块均为系统成功的关键组件。

Abstract: Teleoperating a robotic manipulator in industrial environments demands precision that camera-based interfaces alone struggle to deliver. The operator must align the end-effector with a target in clutter, under limited depth perception, and without colliding with the surrounding structures. This paper presents a shared-autonomy framework that assists the operator throughout this process. A single RGB-D camera captures the operator's arm motion and hand gestures without wearables, fiducials, or a calibration stage. The intended target is specified by a free-form text prompt, grounded by a vision-language model in the robot's gripper camera, and tracked across its onboard cameras by a promptable video-segmentation model, resulting in a grasp frame continuously separated from the obstacle map. Every commanded motion is executed by a GPU-accelerated model-predictive controller that enforces self- and environment-collision avoidance against an online volumetric reconstruction, while a potential field corrects the operator's reference toward the grounded target during the final approach. An autonomous mode can be gesture-triggered to complete the grasp on the same target without a separate perception pipeline. The framework is validated on a quadruped mobile manipulator. The interface achieves a positional RMSE of 59 mm relative to motion-capture ground truth, and the controller keeps the arm at least 18 cm from obstacles while the operator deliberately commands the arm into them by 6 cm. In an industrial valve manipulation and a pick-and-place task, the full framework succeeded in all trials, while ablating either the collision or the assistance module produced failures through complementary mechanisms, and autonomous execution succeeded in four of five trials per task.

</details>


### [74] [Multi-Resolution Voxelized Map-Based Stereo Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17332)
*Shuyi Pan,Hangtian Wang,Zhaoxing Zhang,Chengliang Zhang,Zikang Yuan,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种多分辨率先验地图构建方法及对应的VIO系统，通过锥形索引策略和3D DDA算法实现边缘设备上2D特征与3D地图点的轻量级关联，在保证位姿估计精度的同时大幅降低数据传输量和计算负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先验地图能提升VIO位姿估计的精度和鲁棒性，但地图数据量大且传输带宽有限，无法在边缘设备上持续加载局部地图，亟需一种高效的地图表示与数据关联方法。

Method: 1) 构建多分辨率体素化先验地图，每个体素仅保留一个地图点；2) 在线VIO运行时，采用锥形索引策略将边缘设备的2D特征与3D地图点关联，锥体截距由当前位置到3D点的距离决定；3) 通过3D数字微分分析(DDA)算法检索对应体素内的唯一地图点，选择合适的分辨率层级。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，该系统在实现精确位姿估计的同时，所需的数据传输量极小。

Conclusion: 多分辨率先验地图结合锥形索引与DDA算法，有效解决了地图数据量与传输带宽的矛盾，实现了高效、轻量的地图辅助VIO系统。

Abstract: Incorporating prior maps significantly enhances the accuracy and robustness of pose estimation in visual-inertial odometry (VIO). However, the large data volume of such maps, combined with limited transmission bandwidth, makes it impractical to continuously load local maps onto an edge device. In this paper, we propose a multi-resolution prior map construction method and a corresponding map-based VIO system. The prior map is voxelized at multiple resolutions, with each voxel retaining only a single map point. During online VIO operation, a cone-shaped indexing strategy associates 2D features on the edge device with 3D map points. The cone's intercept is determined by the distance from the current position to the 3D points, enabling the selection of the appropriate resolution level and the retrieval of the unique map point within the corresponding voxel via a 3D digital differential analyzer (DDA) algorithm. This approach minimizes both the volume of data required for transmission and the computational load during data association. Extensive experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that our system achieves accurate pose estimation while requiring minimal data transmission.

</details>


### [75] [Test-Time Scaling for World Action Models via Zero-Shot Geometric Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17454)
*Zesen Zhao,Minkyoung Cho,Hui shen,Boyuan Zheng,Kunxiao Gao,Yulong Cao,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WAMs的免训练选择性测试时扩展框架，通过动作-未来一致性门控机制和跨视图深度重投影一致性筛选，在仅触发26.2%决策点额外采样的情况下，恢复74.8%的全时扩展成功率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人控制需要在执行动作前判断额外计算是否有效，而现有测试时扩展方法未解决此选择性决策问题。

Method: 固定预算的Best-of-N选择器（\method）利用冻结几何基础模型计算跨视图深度重投影一致性排序采样轨迹；\methodgated添加轻量级动作-未来一致性门控，仅在初始轨迹内部不一致时调用\method。

Result: 在RoboCasa、LIBERO Long、RoboTwin 2.0五个基准上，\method在N=8时始终提升任务成功率（如RoboCasa从66.3%到68.4%）；\methodgated以26.2%的额外采样触发率恢复74.8%的全时增益。

Conclusion: 跨视图重投影是有效的无标签选择器；识别出假低分选择这一失败模式，解释了性能随N增大饱和或下降的原因。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves foundation-model inference by spending additional computation, but robot control requires deciding whether extra compute is useful before executing an action. World Action Models (WAMs) make this decision natural: each rollout exposes both an action chunk and predicted future observations. We propose \methodgated, a training-free selective test-time scaling framework for WAMs. We first instantiate \method, a fixed-budget Best-of-$N$ selector that ranks sampled rollouts by cross-view depth reprojection consistency of their predicted futures, computed with a frozen geometry foundation model. \methodgated\ adds a lightweight action--future consistency gate that invokes \method\ only when the initial rollout appears internally inconsistent. Across five benchmark--backbone settings on RoboCasa, LIBERO Long, and RoboTwin~2.0, fixed-budget \method\ improves $N{=}8$ task success in every setting, e.g., raising the RoboCasa group average from $66.3\%$ to $68.4\%$ with Cosmos Policy and from $80.8\%$ to $82.5\%$ with X-WAM. With gating enabled, \methodgated\ recovers on average $74.8\%$ of the always-on success gain while triggering additional sampling on only $26.2\%$ of decision points. Offline diagnostics show that cross-view reprojection is a strong task-label-free selector, and we identify false low-score selections as a failure mode that helps explain why performance can saturate or degrade as $N$ increases.

</details>


### [76] [Disturbance-Aware Flight for Aerial Robots in Narrow Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17476)
*Lei Qiang,Tianyu He,Chenyang Sun,Xurui Liu,Miao Wang,Xiaobin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种干扰感知的规划与控制框架（DAPCF），通过在线扰动估计、扰动风险函数和基于电机动力学的非线性模型预测控制器，实现了四旋翼飞行器在狭窄空间（0.6米宽隧道）中的自主飞行，在成功率和飞行效率上均优于人类飞行员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要在控制层面处理气动干扰，而运动规划仅依赖几何约束和固定速度限制，导致在狭窄空间中行为保守或不安全。

Method: 三部分核心方法：1) 双环观测器实时估计六自由度扰动力和力矩；2) 扰动风险函数根据扰动估计自适应调整规划器参考速度；3) 基于电机动力学的非线性模型预测控制器（MDNMPC）进行扰动补偿，实现鲁棒轨迹跟踪。

Result: 对角线长0.39米的四旋翼可穿越0.6米宽的直隧道、斜隧道和弯曲隧道，在成功率和飞行效率上均超越人类飞行员。

Conclusion: 将干扰估计融入规划-控制闭环可显著提升狭窄空间中的飞行性能，验证了扰动感知框架的实用价值。

Abstract: Autonomous flight of aerial robots in narrow space remains challenging due to strong aerodynamic disturbances and limited flying space. Existing approaches mainly address aerodynamic disturbances at the control level, while motion planning typically relies on geometric constraints and fixed speed limits, leading to conservative or unsafe behaviors in confined environments. This paper presents a disturbance-aware planning and control framework (DAPCF) that integrates online disturbance estimation into the planning-control loop for quadrotor flight in narrow space. First, the dual-loop observers estimate 6-degree-of-freedom disturbance forces and torques in real time based on odometry and motor speed measurements. Then, a disturbance risk function is introduced that adaptively modulates the reference speed of the planner based on disturbance estimation, reducing velocity when disturbances exceed a threshold and restoring it under low-disturbance conditions. Finally, a motor-dynamics-based nonlinear model predictive controller (MDNMPC) with disturbance compensation is designed to ensure robust trajectory tracking under perturbed conditions. Experiments demonstrate that a quadrotor with a diagonal length of 0.39~m can traverse straight, sloped, and curved tunnels as narrow as 0.6~m, outperforming human pilots in both success rate and flight efficiency.

</details>


### [77] [GeoWorldAD: Geometry World Action Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17521)
*Songyan Zhang,Jinyuan Tian,Hanbing Li,Daqi Liu,Hao Chen,Wenhui Huang,Fang Li,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Kuiyuan Yang,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoWorldAD通过将轨迹规划锚定在自车对齐的3D空间中，并利用潜在未来几何令牌预测短时域场景演变，在NAVSIM v1和v2上实现了最先进的自动驾驶规划性能，在安全性和效率之间取得了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision/Video-Action模型虽然能从视觉观测学习策略，但缺乏显式的几何基础和对未来空间感知的引导能力，难以在避撞和行驶效率之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出GeoWorldAD框架：1) 将轨迹规划锚定在自车对齐的3D空间中；2) 利用潜在未来几何令牌预测短时域场景演变；3) 通过迭代轨迹优化逐步聚合多尺度当前几何和潜在未来几何信息。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上达到了最先进的性能，验证了显式3D几何基础与未来几何世界建模对安全高效自动驾驶的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式的3D几何基础提供了安全规划的空间约束，而未来几何建模有助于预判周围智能体和自车自由空间的演变，从而在保证安全的前提下减少过于保守的决策。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires both safe and efficient planning decisions in dynamic 3D environments. Although recent Vision/Video-Action models learn policies directly from visual observations and scale well with advances in vision transformers and large-scale training data, they often lack explicit geometric grounding and future-aware spatial guidance, limiting their ability to balance collision avoidance and driving progress. In this work, we propose GeoWorldAD, a geometry world action model that grounds trajectory planning in ego-aligned 3D space and anticipates short-horizon scene evolution with latent future geometry tokens. Present geometry provides essential spatial constraints for safe planning, while future geometry reveals how surrounding agents and ego-centric free space may evolve, reducing overly conservative decisions without sacrificing safety. To efficiently exploit these geometric cues, GeoWorldAD progressively aggregates multi-scale present geometry and latent future geometry through iterative trajectory refinement. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 and v2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of explicit 3D geometry grounding and future geometry world modeling for safe and efficient autonomous driving.

</details>


### [78] [Predicting Grasping Compliance in Robotic Hands through Analytical-Model-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17541)
*Qianwen Zhao,Long Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析模型-informed神经网络（AMINN），用于预测欠驱动机械手在强力交互中的抓取稳定性和工具位移，相比黑盒MLP基线具有更好的物理一致性和预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统抓取研究将抓取视为二元成功/失败问题，但对于强力工具使用场景，抓取柔顺性成为关键因素，现有的建模方法难以描述欠驱动手中耦合运动学、被动力学和接触条件产生的非线性柔顺行为。

Method: 提出分析模型-informed神经网络（AMINN），将解析力学层与数据驱动学习相结合的混合预测模型，用于估计外部载荷下的抓取稳定性和手中工具位移。

Result: 在三指欠驱动机械手上评估，AMINN在不同载荷条件下展现出强预测能力，输出具有物理可解释性；相比黑盒MLP基线，在能量基物理一致性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 该框架推进了机器人操作中的物理可解释学习，有助于在安全关键场景中实现更可靠、更安全、更可信的自主工具使用。

Abstract: In robotic manipulation studies, grasping is often treated as a binary success or failure problem, usually defined by whether the object simply stays in the hand. For forceful tool use, however, this view is insufficient because grasp compliance becomes a critical factor governing how the hand and tool behave under load. Compliance arises from coupled kinematics, grasp configuration, passive mechanics, and contact conditions, producing nonlinear behavior in which deformation and interaction forces influence each other. Understanding this relationship is essential for predictive models of how a grasped tool and a compliant hand jointly respond to external loading. In underactuated hands, these effects are amplified: such designs offer low cost and adaptive grasping, but make compliance behavior more difficult to model and predict. Our goal is therefore to develop a predictive model for grasped tool behavior during forceful interactions. To address this challenge, we introduce an analytical model informed neural network (AMINN), a hybrid predictive model that combines an analytical mechanics layer with data driven learning to estimate grasp stability and in hand tool displacement under external loading. The model is evaluated on a three finger underactuated robotic hand and shows strong predictive capability with mechanically meaningful outputs across diverse loading conditions. Compared with a black box multilayer perceptron baseline, AMINN also achieves better energy based physical consistency. Beyond prediction accuracy alone, this framework advances physically interpretable learning for robotic manipulation and supports more reliable, safer, and more trustworthy autonomous tool use in safety critical settings during forceful interaction.

</details>


### [79] [Finite-Time Curvature-Constrained Vector Field for Saturation-Free Motion Planning of Nonholonomic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17542)
*Zhouru Xiao,Sha Luo,Yang Lu,Héctor García de Marina,Zhenyang Xu,Chaosong Gong,Yaonan Wang,Weijia Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由有限时间曲率约束向量场（FT-C2VF）和无饱和控制律组成的广义运动规划与控制框架，使非完整移动机器人能在有限时间内收敛到目标构型，同时确保曲率连续有界且控制输入不超出执行器限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于向量场的方法无法显式生成满足曲率约束的轨迹，常依赖输入饱和来满足执行器限制，这会破坏稳定性保证并降低闭环性能；此外这些方法通常仅保证渐近收敛，缺乏显式的有限时间收敛界。

Method: 1) 设计FT-C2VF，利用互补增益实现有限时间收敛，并确保积分曲线曲率连续、有界且随径向比单调递减；2) 开发几乎全局C1光滑的无饱和控制器，无需雅可比信息即可跟踪FT-C2VF，且所有控制输入保持在预设执行器限制内；3) 通过动力系统分析建立目标平衡点的几乎全局有限时间稳定性。

Result: 数值仿真表明该方法优于代表性VF方法；户外实验在阿克曼转向车辆上验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提框架为存在曲率约束和执行器限制的非完整移动机器人提供了具有有限时间收敛保证的运动规划与控制解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately steering a robot to a target configuration is fundamental in engineering, yet remains challenging for nonholonomic mobile robots. Vector fields (VFs) provide a natural framework by specifying desired motion directions throughout the workspace and enabling direct integration with feedback control. However, most existing VF-based methods cannot explicitly generate trajectories satisfying curvature constraints. Actuator limits are therefore often enforced by input saturation, which may invalidate stability guarantees and degrade closed-loop performance when not considered in controller design. In addition, these methods usually ensure only asymptotic convergence without an explicit settling-time bound. To address these issues, we propose a generalized motion planning and control framework consisting of a finite-time curvature-constrained vector field (FT-C2VF) and a saturation-free control law. Depending on the motion objective, the framework drives the robot to the target configuration in finite time or through it periodically. First, the FT-C2VF is constructed using complementary gains to achieve finite-time convergence while ensuring that the curvature of its integral curves is continuous, bounded, and monotonically decreasing with the radial ratio. Second, an almost globally C1-smooth, saturation-free controller is developed to track the FT-C2VF without Jacobian information, while keeping all control inputs within prescribed actuator limits. Third, dynamical-systems analysis establishes almost-global finite-time stability of the target equilibrium. Numerical simulations show improved performance over representative VF-based methods, and outdoor experiments on an Ackermann-steered vehicle confirm the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [80] [COLIP-2: Olfaction-Vision-Language Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17559)
*Kordel Kade France*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COLIP-2是一个将嗅觉与视觉、语言对齐的多模态嵌入模型，使机器人能够将检测到的气味与场景中的物体进行概率性关联，并针对边缘端机器人实时应用进行了优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏ImageNet规模的气味-图像配对数据集，现有嗅觉数据的规模和模态对齐不足，亟需新的方法论和数据集来推动嗅觉导向的机器人感知能力发展。

Method: 构建统一的多模态嵌入空间，将分子结构、气体传感器读数、气味描述语言和图像等不同模态的数据共同训练到一个共享表征空间中，并针对边缘端实时运行进行了必要优化。

Result: 通过内部测试验证了COLIP-2架构的有效性，证明了在有限开源嗅觉数据条件下能够实现的感知能力边界。

Conclusion: COLIP-2展示了当前开源嗅觉数据所能支持的最高水平，旨在以此论证构建更大规模、更高质量的嗅觉-视觉配对数据集以及开发新方法学的必要性，为未来的高级嗅觉机器人感知奠定基础。

Abstract: The Contrastive Olfaction-Language-Image Pre-training 2 (COLIP-2) model is a multimodal embeddings space that places olfaction as a first-class citizen among vision and language. Molecular structure, gas-sensor readings, odor-descriptor language, and images are all trained into a single shared representation space, so that a robot can localize a detected aroma to objects in a scene probabilistically. No ImageNet-scale datasets of paired image-scent examples exists which warrants the need for their collection. Our intent with the release of COLIP-2 is to demonstrate the limit of what can be built for robotics with open-sourced olfactory data in order to ground the argument for why new methodologies and datasets are necessary in order to enable advanced olfactory-oriented perception capabilities. We enumerate results from internal testing of the COLIP-2 architecture and make necessary optimizations to run the model at the edge for real-time robotics applications. While developed with robotics in mind, the design of COLIP-2 has been influenced by experts across many disciplines of science in academia and industry, and we hope that the model can be useful in any multimodal domain requiring olfactory intelligence.

</details>


### [81] [Predictive Training with Latent Imagination for Visual Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17574)
*Yancheng Zhu,Wanli Ma,Chen Han,Irvin Haozhe Zhan,Bingfeng Qin,Yixin Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级预测监督方法，通过训练时对LSTM-SRU导航策略的循环状态施加JEPA风格预测和SIGReg正则化，使机器人能隐式学习动态障碍物的短期演化规律，而在推理时完全丢弃预测器，不增加额外计算开销。在仿真和真实环境（Unitree Go2）中显著降低碰撞率，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的足式机器人导航策略仅依赖当前观测和短期记忆进行反应式决策，无法有效预测动态障碍物的未来运动，导致在动态环境中响应滞后、碰撞风险高。

Method: 在LSTM-SRU导航骨干网络上，训练时引入JEPA风格辅助预测器和SIGReg正则化：预测器监督确定性隐藏状态预测其下一时刻状态，使循环状态隐式编码障碍物短期动态信息；推理时完全丢弃预测器，零额外计算开销。

Result: 在仿真和真实动态障碍物导航基准中，该方法显著提高了导航成功率并降低了碰撞率。在Unitree Go2机器人上实现了零样本sim-to-real迁移，在室内外动态环境中无需微调即可表现出与仿真一致的避障行为。

Conclusion: 仅通过训练阶段的预测性监督信号即可有效提升足式机器人在动态环境中的导航性能，无需增加推理时的模型参数或计算量，且具备良好的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning navigation policies for legged robots select actions reactively from current observations and short-term memory, with limited capacity to anticipate how moving obstacles will evolve in the near future. In dynamic environments, this reactivity causes the robot to respond too late because collision risk depends on short-horizon scene structure rather than on current obstacle positions alone. Lightweight predictive supervision applied to the policy's recurrent state during training can encode anticipatory obstacle dynamics without modifying the inference-time controller. We augment a reactive LSTM-SRU navigation backbone with an auxiliary JEPA-style predictor and SIGReg regularization: during training, the predictor supervises the deterministic hidden state to anticipate its own next state; at inference, it is fully discarded, incurring zero additional computational cost. On simulated and real-world navigation benchmarks with dynamic obstacles, our method substantially improves navigation success while reducing collision rates through the predictive training signal alone, without additional inference-time parameters. Real-robot deployment on a Unitree Go2 demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer: the controller navigates cluttered indoor and dynamic outdoor environments without fine-tuning, with evasive behavior consistent with the collision reduction observed in simulation.

</details>


### [82] [Stability and Comfort in Mobile Robot-Pedestrian Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17604)
*Alireza Jafari,Hong-Son Nguyen,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对非完整移动机器人在人群中的导航问题，提出了基于社会力模型和投影时间-碰撞社会力模型的社会感知算法，证明了系统稳定性，并通过实验和统计分析验证了算法能有效提升行人舒适感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在公共场所需保障行人主观安全感，但现有经验研究稀缺；经典导航算法将行人等同于动态障碍物，未显式建模主观人为因素；多数研究局限于全向移动机器人，而实际应用需要非完整移动机器人。

Method: 设计了基于社会力模型和投影时间-碰撞社会力模型的社会感知框架；形式化了非完整移动机器人-行人和非完整移动机器人-障碍物的交互；假设行人有限非被动，证明了系统稳定性；通过最大化舒适度和速度的混合代价函数进行仿真校准；在行人-机器人交互实验中比较两类模型与两种遥控基线，收集行人报告舒适度，并利用统计工具分析调查结果。

Result: 模型是稳定的；与先前研究的基准测试相比，所提方法在研究的指标上具有优势；非完整移动机器人在人群中导航时，所提模型能改善行人舒适度。

Conclusion: 所提出的社会感知算法在保证系统稳定性的同时，有效提升了非完整移动机器人在行人人群中导航时的行人主观舒适感。

Abstract: Mobile robots in public spaces must ensure pedestrians' comfort, and yet empirical studies of walkers' subjective safety are rare. Many classical navigation algorithms do not distinguish the walkers from dynamic obstacles and do not explicitly model subjective human factors. Moreover, most studies focus on holonomic mobile robots, whereas applications demand Nonholonomic Mobile Robots (NMR). This paper develops socially aware algorithms for NMRs, proves the stability, verifies the performance experimentally, and statistically analyzes the reported comfort. We design a framework for NMRs using Social Force Model (SFM) and the projected Time-to-collision Social Force Model (TSFM). We formalize the NMR-pedestrians' and NMR-obstacles' interactions and prove the system's stability, assuming boundedly nonpassive pedestrians. Simulations calibrate the models by maximizing a hybrid cost function of comfort and speed. Pedestrian-robot interaction experiments compare SFM and TSFM to two remote-controlled baselines and collect walkers' reported comfort. Statistical tools analyze survey results collected during the experiments. Benchmarking the algorithms against previous studies highlights the proposed methods' advantage with respect to the studied metrics. Overall, the models are stable and improve pedestrian comfort when an NMR navigates through a pedestrian crowd.

</details>


### [83] [A2RL V\textsubscript{max}: The A2RL autonomous racing dataset for long-range, high-speed perception and multi-vehicle interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17813)
*Marvin Klemp,Dominic Ebner,Cornelius Schröder,Davide Malvezzi,László Turányi,Riccardo Donati,Ilia Schminik,Xia Ning,Yanxin Zhou,Matthew Flagg,Christoph Stiller,Markus Lienkamp,Marko Bertogna,Gergely Bári,Andreas Birk,Ren Jin,Chen Lv,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了A2RL Vmax开源数据集，专为高速自动驾驶和多车交互场景下的感知任务设计。数据集包含近30,000个专业标注的LiDAR点云和RADAR点云，来自2024年阿布扎比自动驾驶赛车联盟比赛，是首个大规模专业标注的自动驾驶赛车LiDAR数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶感知数据集主要集中在结构化城市环境，缺乏针对高速自动驾驶和多车交互场景的专业数据集。

Method: 在2024年A2RL比赛期间，于亚斯码头F1赛道采集数据，涵盖单车不同速度、多车交互及四车决赛场景，提供近30,000个专业标注的LiDAR和RADAR点云数据，并以开发者友好格式发布。

Result: 提供了现成3D检测和跟踪方法的基线评估，结果表明现有方法在高速场景下仍有挑战，需专门方法应对。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了高速自动驾驶感知领域的空白，为深度学习研究提供了标准化基准，但高速场景下的3D感知仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: In autonomous driving development, a perception dataset is crucial, as it provides fundamental data for training, testing, and validating algorithms for an autonomous vehicle's multimodal perception systems. So far, most research has concentrated on providing datasets for well-structured urban environments. This work introduces the A2RL V\textsubscript{max} open-source dataset, specifically designed for perception tasks in high-speed autonomous driving and multi-vehicle interaction. The dataset was captured during the 2024 Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League (A2RL), held at the Yas Marina F1 Circuit, with participation from all competing teams. It contains diverse scenarios, including single-vehicle data at varying speeds, multi-vehicle sessions, and the full final four-vehicle race. The dataset contains almost 30,000 professionally annotated LiDAR point clouds, along with RADAR point clouds. In particular, it is the first large-scale dataset in autonomous racing to feature professionally annotated LiDAR point clouds, enabling deep learning-based perception research. The data is provided in a developer-friendly format, enabling easy implementation and evaluation in future research. We provide implementation and evaluation for off-the-shelf 3D detection and tracking methods. Although baseline methods show promising results for both 3D detection and tracking, specialized methods are required to address the unique challenges of high-speed autonomous driving. For a detailed description of the dataset, please visit the \href{https://tum-avs.github.io/A2RL_Dataset_website/}{A2RL V\textsubscript{max} Dataset Website}

</details>


### [84] [Configuration-Induced Passive Self-Rotation for Perception-Enhanced Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17646)
*Xurui Liu,Miao Wang,Tianyu He,Linrui Yang,Xiaobin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构型驱动的被动自旋三旋翼无人机，通过后臂构型参数调节被动自旋工作点，在无需额外传感器的情况下平衡扫描视场刷新率与飞行性能，并开发了层次化自主飞行框架实现连续被动自旋下的敏捷鲁棒飞行，真实实验验证了其在感知增强自主飞行中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行在受限和杂乱环境中受限于机载传感器的有限视场。被动自旋可扩展感知覆盖范围，但引入了扫描视场刷新率与飞行性能之间的权衡问题。

Method: 1) 利用后臂构型参数调节被动自旋工作点，提供机体层面的机制以平衡扫描视场刷新率与飞行性能；2) 开发了集成规划与控制的层次化自主框架，实现连续被动自旋下的敏捷鲁棒飞行；3) 在航点检查任务中进一步使用引导点重规划提升任务级覆盖率。

Result: 通过真实世界实验验证，包括高速轨迹跟踪、抗干扰测试以及在代表性杂乱环境中的自主导航，证明了该方法在感知增强自主飞行中的有效性。

Conclusion: 构型驱动的被动自旋三旋翼设计能够在不增加额外传感器的情况下有效扩展感知覆盖范围，且所提出的层次化自主框架能够平衡扫描视场刷新率与飞行性能，实现感知增强的自主飞行。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in confined and cluttered environments is fundamentally limited by the restricted field of view (FoV) of onboard sensors. Passive self-rotation expands sensing coverage without additional sensors but introduces a tradeoff between swept-FoV refresh rate and flight performance. This letter presents a configuration-induced passively self-rotating tricopter for perception-enhanced autonomous flight. Firstly, the rear-arm configuration parameter is exploited to regulate the passive self-rotation operating point, providing an airframe-level mechanism for balancing swept-FoV refresh rate and flight performance. Secondly, a hierarchical autonomy framework integrating planning and control is developed to enable agile and robust autonomous flight under continuous passive self-rotation. For waypoint-based inspection, guide-point replanning is further used to improve task-level coverage. Extensive real-world experiments, including high-speed trajectory tracking, disturbance-rejection tests, and autonomous navigation in representative cluttered environments, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for perception-enhanced autonomous flight.

</details>


### [85] [HCPG-Flow:Hierarchical Contact-Progress Guidance for Flow-Policy Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17651)
*Guanghu Xie,Mingxu Li,Shuo Zhang,Yonglong Zhang,Yifan Yang,Yang Liu,Zongwu Xie,Baoshi Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HCPG-Flow 提出了一种分层物体感知的接触-进度引导选择器，用于机器人操作中基于流的策略，通过分析性滚动时间选择替代基于评论家的排序，在十个模拟任务和四个物理任务上提升了成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的策略（如SAC-Flow）在动作选择时依赖评论家对候选动作的价值估计进行排序，但那些动作在经验回放中可能表征较弱，导致数据收集质量下降。

Method: HCPG-Flow 在SAC-Flow基础上引入分层物体感知的接触-进度引导选择器：接触前使用末端执行器趋近策略，接触后切换为任务进度引导；对每个候选动作计算任务相关距离的一阶缩减量作为评分，在候选集内标准化分数，并执行温度控制的动作嵌入。

Result: 在十个模拟任务中，HCPG在两个基准测试上的平均成功率均优于SAC-Flow，其中Maniskill提升9.5个百分点；四个物理任务中成功率高，完成任务步骤数减少17.4%。

Conclusion: HCPG-Flow通过分析性选择器替代评论家排序，在保持原有演员-评论家目标的同时，有效提升了基于流的策略在机器人操作任务中的性能和效率。

Abstract: Flow policies can represent multimodal action distributions for robot manipulation, yet a robot must execute one action at each control step. When several proposals are sampled, critic-based ranking makes data collection depend on value estimates over candidate actions that may be weakly represented in replay. We introduce HCPG-Flow, an analytic rollout-time selector that augments SAC-Flow with hierarchical, object-centric contact-progress guidance while preserving its actor and critic objectives. HCPG switches from end-effector approach to task progress after contact, scores each proposal by the first-order reduction of a task-relevant distance, standardizes scores within the candidate set, and executes a temperature-controlled action embedding. Across ten simulated tasks, HCPG improves mean success over SAC-Flow on both benchmarks, including a 9.5 percentage-point gain on Maniskill. Four physical tasks further show high success with a 17.4% reduction in successful completion steps.Project page: https://hitxraz.github.io/HCPG-Flow/

</details>


### [86] [SLAM in Low-Light Environments: Project Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17699)
*Oleh Basystyi,Anna Stasyshyn,Oleksandr Kosovan,Yaroslav Prytula*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在低光照条件下系统基准测试了六种RGB-SLAM系统（涵盖特征法、直接法、滤波法和学习法），发现仅有融合IMU与全局优化的Kimera-VIO能完整跟踪所有序列，而经典单目和滤波方法在低光下普遍失效，表明低光RGB-SLAM的鲁棒性依赖于惯性融合与全局优化的共同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照条件下，对比度降低、传感器噪声和运动模糊严重退化特征提取与匹配，而使用LiDAR、深度或热感传感器会增加成本、功耗和集成复杂度。现有基准测试多集中于良好光照场景，缺乏对RGB相机在黑暗环境中SLAM性能极限的系统评估。

Method: 在五个难度和光照条件不同的LaMARia序列上，基准测试了六种SLAM系统：ORB-SLAM3（特征法）、DSO（直接法）、Kimera-VIO（融合IMU）、OpenVINS（滤波法）、DPVO和DPV-SLAM（学习法），报告了绝对/相对位姿误差和控制点召回率。

Result: Kimera-VIO是唯一完整跟踪所有五个序列的系统，具有最低的相对位姿误差但绝对误差持续增长（缺乏闭环）；DPVO和DPV-SLAM从未丢失跟踪但低光下绝对误差约100米；ORB-SLAM3、DSO和OpenVINS在大多数困难及低光序列上失败或发散。

Conclusion: RGB-only SLAM在低光照下保持稳定跟踪仅当同时具备惯性融合和全局优化。要弥合剩余差距，可能需要低光专用的学习前端或回归互补感知方案。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is one of the fundamental problems in robotics, as it enables autonomous operations in real-world scenarios. Under low illumination, reduced contrast, sensor noise, and motion blur degrade both feature extraction and feature matching, while compensating with LiDAR, depth, or thermal sensors raises cost, power draw, and integration complexity. Existing benchmarks remain dominated by well-lit indoor or daylight sequences, leaving open how far SLAM with standard RGB cameras can be pushed in the dark. We benchmark six systems spanning the feature-based, direct, filter-based, and learning-based paradigms - ORB-SLAM3, DSO, Kimera-VIO, OpenVINS, DPVO, and DPV-SLAM - on five LaMARia sequences of varying difficulty and illumination, reporting absolute and relative pose error alongside control-point recall. Kimera-VIO is the only system to track all five sequences to completion, combining the lowest relative pose error with steadily growing absolute error due to the absence of loop closure; DPVO and DPV-SLAM never lose tracking but incur absolute errors of roughly 100 m under low light; and the classical monocular pipelines (ORB-SLAM3, DSO) together with the filter-based OpenVINS fail outright or diverge on most of the harder and low-light sequences. The results suggest that RGB-only SLAM maintains stable low-light tracking only when both inertial fusion and global optimization are present. Closing the remaining gap will likely require low-light-specific learned front-ends or a return to complementary sensing.

</details>


### [87] [Lifelong Multi-Subsystem Pickup and Delivery with Buffer-Limited Handover Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17724)
*Chuanlong Zang,Isabelle Barz,Anna Mannucci,Philipp Schillinger,Florian Lier,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对多子系统MAPD中带有限缓冲区的交接站协调问题，提出了HARR在线控制器，通过共享停靠日历和滚动时域缓冲占用预测来实现子系统间协调，在仿真中相比基线方法实现了高达77%的吞吐量提升和92%的后积压减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体拾取与运送（MAPD）系统中，子系统间通过共享手拉式交接站（配备单个停靠点和有限缓冲区）进行负载传输时，容易发生阻塞和饥饿问题，现有方法缺乏对这类接口的显式协调机制。

Method: 提出HARR（交接感知预留与路由）在线控制器，包含共享停靠日历和确定性滚动时域缓冲占用预测，候选路径需通过停靠间隔空闲性和缓冲容量约束检查方可接受。

Result: 在中等负载下，HARR相比固定停靠消融实验实现77%的吞吐量提升和92%的后积压降低，同时相比耦合的站感知Token Passing基线减少了规划时间。

Conclusion: 显式的接口协调能显著提升模块化多子系统运输系统的稳定性，HARR为这类问题提供了有效的在线协调框架。

Abstract: Coordinating payload transfers between subsystems is a critical challenge in lifelong Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD). We study systems where agents are confined to separate regions and must exchange payloads through shared handover stations. These stations, equipped with single docks and finite buffers, are inherently vulnerable to blocking and starvation. We formalize this problem as Multi-Subsystem MAPD with Buffer-limited Handover Stations (MS-MAPD-BHS). We then propose Handover-Aware Reservation and Routing (HARR), an online controller that couples per-subsystem planners. HARR uses a shared dock reservation calendar and a deterministic rolling-horizon projection of buffer occupancy to coordinate actions. A candidate route is accepted only if its dock interval is free and the resulting buffer occupancy projection remains within capacity. Under perfect execution, these checks ensure collision-free dock use and buffer-safe committed operations within the reservation horizon. In simulation, HARR achieves up to 77% higher throughput and 92% lower backlog than a fixed-dock ablation at moderate load, while also reducing planning time relative to a coupled station-aware Token Passing baseline. These results show that explicit interface coordination substantially improves stability in modular multi-subsystem transport.

</details>


### [88] [Seg2Grasp: A Robust Modular Suction Grasping in Bin Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17757)
*Hye-Jung Yoon,Juno Kim,Yesol Park,Jun-Ki Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Seg2Grasp提出了一种模块化的吸盘抓取流水线，包含分割、抓取和分类三个步骤，通过Transformer分割、表面法线吸盘点预测和开放词汇分类，在动态杂乱料箱场景中显著提升了抓取成功率和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于端到端学习的料箱抓取方法在面对非熟悉或复杂物体时表现不佳，鲁棒性不足，难以适应非结构化环境中的动态杂乱料箱场景。

Method: 采用模块化流水线设计：1) 分割模块使用Transformer模型从RGB-D图像生成类别无关的物体掩码；2) 抓取模块利用表面法线和掩码提议确定最优吸盘点；3) 分类模块通过微调开放词汇Mask-CLIP实现精确物体识别。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，Seg2Grasp在成功率和适应性方面均优于现有方法，能够有效处理多样化的物体。

Conclusion: Seg2Grasp作为一种模块化方法，有效弥补了端到端学习方法在泛化性上的不足，为工业自动化料箱抓取提供了一种鲁棒且适应性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Current bin picking methods that rely heavily on end-to-end learning often falter when confronted with unfamiliar or complex objects in unstructured environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Seg2Grasp, a modular pipeline designed for robust suction grasping in dynamic and cluttered bin scenarios. Seg2Grasp is built on a three-step process: Segmentation, Grasping, and Classification. The Segmentation module employs a Transformer-based model to generate class-agnostic object masks from RGB-D images, ensuring accurate detection across various conditions. The Grasping module uses surface normals and mask proposals to determine the optimal suction points, enhancing grasp success. Finally, the Classification module leverages fine-tuned open-vocabulary Mask-CLIP for precise object identification, enabling versatile handling of diverse objects. Real-world robotic experiments demonstrate that Seg2Grasp outperforms existing methods in success rates and adaptability, establishing it as a powerful tool for automated bin picking in industrial settings.

</details>


### [89] [VLN-AVP: Zero-Shot Vision-Language Navigation with Hybrid Long-Short-Term Memory for Autonomous Valet Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17767)
*Yijian Li,Xiangru Mu,Changze Li,Hantian Shi,Jiyuan Cai,Jia Cai,Xiaoxue Liu,Yajing Sun,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLN-AVP，一种零样本自主代客泊车导航框架，通过融合BEV模型的精准空间感知与VLM的通用智能，消除了对预建地图的依赖，并引入混合记忆系统（短期感知记忆+长期拓扑记忆）来提升语义理解与策略学习。同时构建了首个地下停车场VLN基准数据集，实验显示在仿真中成功率比VLN方法提升25%以上，比自动驾驶方法提升15%以上，并在真实车辆实验中取得了领先的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主代客泊车方法严重依赖预建地图，限制了其在未见环境和开放词汇目标下的可扩展性。

Method: 提出VLN-AVP零样本导航框架，结合BEV模型的精准空间感知与VLM的通用智能；引入混合记忆系统（短期感知记忆追踪语义视觉线索，长期拓扑记忆稳定策略学习）；构建VLN-AVP数据集与基准（10个高保真停车场场景，超1000个导航片段）。

Result: 仿真中成功率比VLN方法提升25%以上，比自动驾驶方法提升15%以上；真实车辆实验中取得领先成功率。

Conclusion: VLN-AVP通过消除对预建地图的依赖并融合VLM与BEV，有效提升了AVP在未见环境下的零样本导航能力，且具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Existing methods in Autonomous Valet Parking (AVP) typically rely on pre-built maps, which severely restricts their scalability to unseen environments and open-vocabulary targets. Inspired by the application of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) tasks, we propose VLN-AVP, a zero-shot navigation framework for AVP tasks. By combining the precise spatial perception of a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) model with the general intelligence of VLMs, our framework 1) eliminates the dependency on pre-built maps, 2) interprets semantic environmental contexts in parking scenarios, and 3) enables intuitive navigation following natural language instructions. Specifically, we introduce a hybrid memory system: a short-term perception memory tracks semantic visual cues to address the limitations of VLM's single-frame reasoning in existing methods, while a long-term topological memory facilitates stable policy learning from past experiences. To bridge the gap in existing benchmarks, we also present the VLN-AVP dataset and benchmark. Featuring 10 high-fidelity parking scenes and over 1,000 navigation episodes, it has the largest number of garage scenes to date and is the first VLN benchmark for underground parking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that in simulation, our method achieves an over 25% improvement in success rate compared to VLN methods and an over 15% improvement compared to other autonomous driving methods. Furthermore, it attains a leading success rate in real-world vehicle experiments, proving its practical feasibility.

</details>


### [90] [From Sign Language Generation to Humanoid Execution: Vision-Language Guided Retargeting with Collision Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17769)
*Nabeela Khan,Bowen Wu,Runwu Shi,Benjamin Yen,Takeshi Ashizawa,Carlos Toshinori Ishi,Takashi Minato,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统级框架，通过体积SMPL-X碰撞缓解模块和视觉-语言引导的重定向算法，解决手语生成中的自相交问题，实现从生成运动到人形机器人关节空间执行的安全映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语生成系统输出的密集3D身体表示在机器人执行时因自相交（手-手、手-躯干穿透）导致逆运动学无解、碰撞和轨迹不稳定，而离线渲染中可容忍的伪影在实体执行中变得致命。

Method: 方法包含两个组件：(1) 体积SMPL-X碰撞缓解模块——将生成的手语运动投影到物理可行构型，同时最小化轨迹偏离；(2) 视觉-语言引导的重定向算法——基于IK骨架，利用VLM作为视觉评判器识别具身失败模式并触发任务空间修正。

Result: 碰撞处理和感知引导的精化是人形机器人可靠手语执行的关键缺失组件，实验验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 系统级框架通过碰撞缓解和VLM驱动的视觉反馈，弥合了手语生成输出与人形机器人关节空间执行之间的差距，显著提升了物理可行性。

Abstract: Recent sign language generation (SLG) systems increasingly output dense 3D body representations, which better preserve full-body kinematics and geometry for downstream embodiment on humanoid robots. However, these generated motions frequently exhibit self-intersections such as hand-hand and hand-torso penetration. While such artifacts may be tolerated in offline rendering, they become critical in humanoid execution as they lead to infeasible inverse-kinematics (IK) solutions, collisions, and unstable retargeted trajectories. We present a system-level framework that bridges SLG outputs to humanoid joint-space execution via two components. First, we introduce a volumetric SMPL-X collision-mitigation module that projects generated signing motions toward physically plausible configurations while minimally deviating from the original trajectory. Second, we propose a vision-language-guided retargeting algorithm built on an IK backbone: a VLM serves as a visual critic over rendered humanoid motion, identifies embodiment-specific failure modes, and triggers targeted task-space corrections. Our results highlight collision handling and perception-guided refinement as key missing components for reliable humanoid signing.

</details>


### [91] [Polar Coordinate-based Differential Evolution for Moving Target Search Using Vision Sensor on Unmanned Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17771)
*Thu Hang Khuat,Duy-Nam Bui,Thuy Ngan Duong,Manh Duong Phung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于极坐标差分进化(PDE)的无人机搜索算法，通过马尔可夫过程建模目标动态、贝叶斯理论估计目标位置概率分布，并利用极坐标引入无人机运动学约束，在检测概率和执行时间上优于现有算法，经仿真和真实无人机实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在搜索救援任务中，"黄金时间"内找到存活目标的概率最高，现有算法在检测概率和执行时间方面仍有提升空间，需要一种更高效的无人机搜索路径规划方法。

Method: 首先将目标动态建模为马尔可夫过程，检测似然函数由图像质量和观察者视觉决定；然后利用贝叶斯理论推导表示目标位置概率分布的适应度函数；最后提出极坐标差分进化(PDE)算法，利用极坐标编码融入无人机的运动学约束和机动特性以生成最大化适应度函数的搜索路径。

Result: 仿真和真实无人机实验结果表明，PDE算法在多种搜索场景下的检测概率和执行时间均优于现有最先进算法，且具备实际应用可行性。

Conclusion: 极坐标差分进化算法能有效提升搜索救援任务中无人机搜索的效率，算法开源代码已公开。

Abstract: In search and rescue operations, there is a period known as the "golden time" during which the probability of finding the target alive is highest. The objective of this work is to propose a new search algorithm for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) with a focus on improving the detection probability and execution time. We approach this problem by first modeling target dynamics as a Markov process and the detection likelihood as a function of image quality and the observer's vision. We then employ Bayesian theory to derive a fitness function representing the probability distribution of the target's location over the search area. Finally, we introduce a new algorithm named polar coordinate-based differential evolution (PDE) to generate a UAV search path that maximizes this fitness function. The PDE algorithm utilizes polar coordinates to incorporate kinematic constraints and maneuver properties of the UAV, allowing for better exploration of the solution space. A series of simulations and comparative analyses have been conducted to evaluate the performance of the proposed algorithm. Experiments involving a real UAV have also been conducted. Results demonstrate that the PDE algorithm outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms in terms of detection probability and execution time across diverse search scenarios while remaining practical for real-world applications. The source code of the algorithm is available at https://github.com/thuhangkhuat/PDE_target_search.

</details>


### [92] [Reasoning as a Double-Edged Sword: Architecture and Cross-Stage Robustness in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17786)
*Tuan Duong Trinh,Naveed Akhtar,Basim Azam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了推理步骤对VLA模型鲁棒性的影响，发现潜在迭代推理模型在扰动下任务成功率急剧下降，而文本链式推理和无推理模型表现更稳健；同时，基于推理输出的安全监控在自适应攻击下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直觉上，在行动前进行推理的策略应能更好地吸收扰动输入，但这一假设尚未经过系统验证。本文旨在探究推理设计是否真的能提升VLA模型对扰动的鲁棒性。

Method: 选取三种覆盖不同推理谱系的VLA模型（无推理、文本链式推理、潜在迭代推理），分别在视觉、推理和行动阶段施加随机噪声和白盒扰动，在LIBERO和SimplerEnv两个基准上评估任务成功率；同时设计基于推理输出的安全监控机制，并进行自适应攻击下的公平测试。

Result: 潜在迭代推理模型在两种扰动下任务成功率急剧下降，远不如无推理和文本链式推理模型稳健；推理深度对鲁棒性影响极小。基于推理输出的监控在朴素评估下表现良好，但在自适应攻击下失效；与动作异常探针融合后，在匹配FPR校准下未能提升防御后的成功率。

Conclusion: 推理设计并不能自动带来鲁棒性提升，潜在迭代推理反而引入结构脆弱性；基于推理输出的行为级监控存在根本性上限，任何可行的防御必须首先满足这一前提条件。

Abstract: Does adding a reasoning step make a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model more robust to perturbation? Intuitively, a policy that reasons before acting should absorb a perturbed input better than one that maps observations directly to actions. We test this premise head-on across three models that span the reasoning spectrum (no reasoning, a text chain-of-thought, and a latent iterative loop), perturbing each at the vision, reasoning, and action stages on LIBERO and SimplerEnv. Two questions organize the study: does the reasoning design shift robustness, and can the reasoning be read back at runtime as a safety signal? We find that the latent-iterative model is by far the least robust: under both stochastic noise and white-box perturbation its task success collapses, while the other two hold. This fragility is structural rather than cumulative: varying the reasoning depth at inference barely moves it. Reasoning outputs can in principle be monitored, but the monitors fail under fair tests. A plan--action consistency probe that looks near-perfect under naive evaluation falls to chance under adaptive attack. Under matched-FPR calibration, fusing it with an action-anomaly probe never lifts defended success above undefended. Scoped to these output-level behavioral probes under white-box vision-stage attack, this ceiling is a precondition that any viable defense must first satisfy.

</details>


### [93] [Task-Space Constrained Stochastic Trajectory Optimization for Time-Optimal Forestry Crane Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17818)
*Marc-Philip Ecker,Christoph Fröhlich,Bernhard Bischof,Wolfgang Kemmetmüller,Tobias Glück*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TSC-VP-STO算法，扩展了VP-STO，通过将终端关节空间约束替换为任务空间约束，联合优化轨迹和冗余自由度，在液压泵流量约束下实现林业起重机更高效、碰撞-free且时间最优的运动规划，实验表明轨迹时长平均减少12-15%，泵利用率提升，并在真实林业起重机上成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VP-STO算法在林业起重机的准时间最优混合运动规划中表现良好，但其需要预先指定固定的终端关节构型。对于运动学冗余的林业起重机，这种对单一逆运动学解的预先承诺限制了规划器利用冗余的能力，特别是在非线性、全局耦合的泵流量约束下（可容许的关节速度取决于其组合液压需求），这一限制尤为突出。

Method: TSC-VP-STO：任务空间约束扩展的VP-STO。用任务空间约束替换严格的终端关节空间约束，联合优化轨迹和终端构型的冗余自由度。通过构型空间分解进行形式化，并推导了林业起重机运动学的具体可达性约束。

Result: 在多个规划目标和路径点配置下，轨迹时长平均减少12-15%，泵流量利用率相比基线VP-STO有所提升。通过真实林业起重机上的完整原木装载循环验证了实际适用性。

Conclusion: TSC-VP-STO通过任务空间约束释放了冗余自由度，使规划器能根据环境依赖的运动和液压流量分配自适应调整终端构型，实现更均衡的泵利用率和更短的轨迹时长，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Efficient, collision-free, and time-optimal motion planning is a fundamental requirement for autonomous forestry cranes operating under hydraulic pump-flow constraints. The Via-Point-based Stochastic Trajectory Optimization (VP-STO) algorithm has demonstrated near-time-optimal hybrid motion planning in this domain, but requires a fixed terminal joint configuration specified prior to optimization. For kinematically redundant manipulators such as forestry cranes, this pre-commitment to a single inverse kinematics solution restricts the planner's ability to exploit redundancy, particularly under the nonlinear, globally coupled pump-flow constraint where admissible joint velocities depend on their combined hydraulic demand. This paper presents TSC-VP-STO, a task-space-constrained extension of VP-STO that replaces the strict terminal joint-space constraint with a task-space constraint, jointly optimizing the trajectory and the redundant degrees of freedom of the terminal configuration. This enables the planner to adapt end configurations to the environment-dependent motion and hydraulic flow allocation, yielding more balanced pump utilization and shorter trajectory durations. We formalize the approach through a configuration space decomposition and derive a concrete reachability constraint for the forestry crane kinematics. Experimental evaluations across multiple planning targets and via-point configurations demonstrates a reduction on trajectory durations by 12-15% on average and improved pump-flow utilization compared to the baseline VP-STO. The practical applicability of TSC-VP-STO is validated through real-world deployment on a forestry crane, including a full log-loading cycle.

</details>


### [94] [Receiver-Centered Robot-to-Human Handover with Grasp-Aware Object Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17839)
*Federico Biagi,Dario Onfiani,Simone Silenzi,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对非对称工业工具的传统静态交接常导致抓取不良的问题，本文提出了一种基于语音驱动、接收者为中心的自适应工具交接系统。该系统利用LLM进行意图识别，MediaPipe进行实时3D手部追踪，动态调整末端执行器方向以呈现符合人体工程学的最佳握持姿态。实验表明，该方法减少了非对称工具的抓取延迟，提升了交互流畅性，并改善了运动可预测性和任务简易性等信任感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态工具交接在处理非对称工业工具时，往往导致接收者抓取姿态别扭，影响操作流畅性和安全性。本文旨在解决这一人机协作中的关键微交互问题。

Method: 以接收者为中心，结合LLM（大语言模型）进行意图识别与MediaPipe进行实时3D手部追踪，动态调整Franka协作机器人末端执行器姿态，将工具以手柄朝前的最优人体工学姿态呈现给操作者。

Result: 在受试者内实验设计中，自适应方法相比对象无关的静态基线，显著减少了非对称工具的抓取延迟，提升了交互流畅性；同时在运动可预测性和任务简易性等信任感知维度上表现更优。

Conclusion: 语音驱动的自适应交接策略能有效改善非对称工业工具的人机交接体验，在提高效率的同时增强了操作者的信任感，为人机协作中的安全高效交互提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Collaborative robots are increasingly sharing workspaces with human operators, making tool handover a frequent and safety-critical micro-interaction. However, traditional static handovers often lead to awkward grasps when handling asymmetric industrial tools. This paper presents a receiver-centered voice-driven adaptive handover system for mechanical tools, built on a Franka cobot. Using an LLM for intention recognition and MediaPipe for real-time 3D hand tracking, the framework dynamically adjusts the end-effector's orientation to present tools in an ergonomically optimal, handle-first pose. A within-subjects study compared this adaptive approach with an object-agnostic static baseline. The results demonstrate that the adaptive system reduces the grasp delay for asymmetric tools, improving the fluency of the interaction. Furthermore, the adaptive strategy improved specific trust-related perceptions, particularly motion predictability and perceived task simplicity.

</details>


### [95] [Lifelong Localization in Dynamic Indoor Environments Combining Odometry with Sparse Distance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17852)
*Michael M. Bilevich,Tomer Buber,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在动态平面室内环境中，利用机器人里程计和稀疏距离采样实现鲁棒终身定位的框架。该方法能实时解决“被绑架机器人”问题，仅需16个距离样本即可达到与SLAM相当的定位效果，在传感器成本、隐私、存储和传输带宽方面具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在现实场景中常面临不可预见的动态障碍物，导致预建地图不准确，传统定位方法失效。需要一种能够在动态环境中鲁棒地实现终身定位的方法，同时降低对传感器成本和数据量的依赖。

Method: 利用稀疏距离采样提供机器人位置的鲁棒先验，结合里程计信息进行时序融合。通过先验分布逐步收敛到机器人真实位姿，并针对动态障碍物进行建模。理论证明在静态环境下该方法能收敛到真实位姿，在动态环境下若变化性质被正确学习也能保证收敛。

Result: 在多个真实室内环境中，仅使用16个距离样本即可达到与完整LiDAR SLAM相当的定位精度，并能实时解决“被绑架机器人”问题（至多存在对称性模糊）。

Conclusion: 稀疏距离采样结合里程计融合是一种高效、低成本的鲁棒定位方案，在动态室内环境中具有收敛性保证和实际可行性。

Abstract: Localization is a key task in robot navigation, and many techniques exist for it. In many plausible scenarios, a robot might face unforeseen, dynamic obstacles, rendering any pre-determined map inaccurate for localization. In this work, we propose a robust lifelong localization framework in dynamic planar indoor environments, using the robot's odometry and sparse distance sampling. We demonstrate how distance samples can be used to provide a robust prior on the robot's location. This technique can solve the kidnapped robot problem in real time, up to symmetries. Based on insights from real-world recorded data, we also account for dynamic obstacles. We then fuse this prior, over time, with the odometry to converge to the robot's location. A central property of our method is that it provably converges to the robot's ground truth pose even in large indoor environments when the environment is static. We further show that this guarantee also holds in dynamic environments, as long as the nature of those changes has been correctly learned. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in different real-world indoor environments. In particular, we achieve a localization comparable to SLAM with merely a few (sixteen) distance samples, as opposed to the full LiDAR range. Sufficing with only sparse distance sampling is advantageous in terms of sensor cost, privacy, storage space, and transmission bandwidth.

</details>


### [96] [ConceptTree: Bringing Semantic Transparency to Black-Box Decision Making for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17861)
*Yongyan Wen,Feifan Liu,Jinyi Chen,Bo An,Peng Liu,Siyuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConceptTree提出了一种将长程机器人操作中的高层技能选择重构为基于可解释概念推理的框架，通过视觉输入构建归一化概念空间并训练决策树，实现了透明、可追溯且可干预的决策过程，在真实机器人任务中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作方法将技能选择视为从观察到动作的不透明映射，缺乏决策过程的透明性，难以实现可靠的人类监督和干预。

Method: 提出ConceptTree框架，将高层操作技能选择重构为基于人类可解释概念的推理过程，学习基于视觉输入的归一化概念空间，并在其上训练决策树来预测高层技能，形成透明、可追溯且可干预的决策流程。

Result: 在真实世界机器人操作任务中，ConceptTree持续优于现有基于概念的基线方法，尤其在复杂长程场景中表现突出。定性案例研究表明模型支持通过修改单个概念进行细粒度干预，无需重新训练即可纠正决策错误。

Conclusion: ConceptTree通过将高层策略表示为视觉观察上的概念级谓词序列，实现了可解释、可追溯和可干预的决策过程，为长程机器人操作中的人类监督与干预提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Establishing interpretable decision-making processes in long-horizon robotic manipulation is critical for enabling reliable human oversight and intervention. However, existing approaches to robotic manipulation largely treat skill selection as opaque mappings from observations to actions, offering limited transparency into how decisions are formed. In this work, we propose ConceptTree, a framework that reframes high-level manipulation skill selection as reasoning over human-interpretable concepts, representing high-level policies as a sequence of concept-level predicates over visual observations. Rather than relying on implicit latent representations, our method learns a normalized concept space grounded in visual inputs, over which a decision tree is trained to predict high-level skills. This formulation yields a transparent decision process that is both traceable and intervenable, enabling direct inspection and modification of policy behavior. We evaluate our approach on a set of real-world robotic manipulation tasks with increasing complexity. Experimental results show that ConceptTree consistently outperforms existing concept-based baselines, particularly in complex, long-horizon scenarios. Furthermore, we provide qualitative case studies showing that our model supports fine-grained intervention by modifying individual concepts, enabling targeted correction of decision errors without retraining.

</details>


### [97] [Leveraging Two Robotic Arms for Tight Assembly Performance Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17876)
*Dror Livnat,Yuval Lavi,Michael M. Bilevich,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向双机械臂紧密装配任务的全流程框架，利用零件CAD模型和装配目标位姿，实现从规划到执行的一体化。相比单臂系统，双臂协调可将平均执行时间降低50%以上，同时提升轨迹质量与有效位姿搜索速度，并给出了机器人工作单元尺寸的理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单臂装配系统在处理紧密装配任务时效率较低，且难以保证执行时间与轨迹精度的理论保障。利用双臂协同有望突破单臂的物理与运动学限制，但缺乏端到端的系统化框架及定量对比分析。

Method: 提出端到端框架：输入零件CAD模型与装配目标位姿，由双机械臂协同执行装配操作。同时从理论上给出了执行时间、轨迹精度的保证，并对机器人工作单元所需尺寸建立了数学界限。方法在仿真与实物平台上均进行了验证。

Result: 双臂协调相比单臂：平均执行时间减少超过50%；轨迹质量更高；有效位姿搜索速度更快。实验同时支撑了执行时间和轨迹精度的理论保证。

Conclusion: 双机械臂协同在紧密装配任务中显著优于单臂系统，本框架提供了理论保障与开源实现，为实际工业应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We provide a novel end-to-end framework for the execution of an assembly operation by two robotic arms, given the digital CAD models of the parts and their desired relative placement in their assembled state. We analyze and demonstrate the advantages of using two robotic arms simultaneously in tight assembly operations, compared to single-arm systems. Our method is implemented in both simulation and using physical robots. It provides theoretical guarantees on execution time and trajectory accuracy, supported by empirical evidence. In particular, we show that coordinated movement of two arms reduces average execution time by more than 50% compared to using a single arm only, produces higher-quality trajectories, and accelerates the search for valid robot placements. Furthermore, we establish bounds on the required dimensions of the robotic cell. Our open source software together with real-life video demonstrations are available in our project page.

</details>


### [98] [Manifold-Guided Motion Planning for Tight Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17898)
*Dror Livnat,Michael M. Bilevich,Michal Kleinbort,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CMG-RRT算法，通过自适应偏向对临界流形（零件间至少有一个接触点的构型子集）邻域的采样，解决刚体装配中因紧几何约束导致的运动规划难题。该算法具有概率完备性，在旋转装配基准测试中达到100%成功率，并首次全自动解出了Elk解缠谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体装配运动规划面临紧几何约束的挑战，可行运动通常需要穿过（接近）零间隙构型，此时零件受接触紧密约束。现有规划方法难以有效处理此类问题。

Method: 提出临界流形引导的RRT（CMG-RRT），核心观察是紧装配中有效解路径位于或靠近临界流形（至少有一个接触点的构型子集）。算法通过构型空间的层次化细分自适应偏向对临界流形邻域的采样。

Result: 在挑战性旋转装配基准测试中达到100%成功率，包括首次全自动解出Elk解缠谜题。

Conclusion: CMG-RRT是专为紧装配问题设计的采样规划器，具有概率完备性；实验证明其有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Motion planning for rigid-body assembly poses a fundamental challenge in robotics due to tight geometric constraints. In such scenarios, feasible motions often require passing through (near-)zero clearance configurations in which the parts are tightly constrained by contact. In this work, we introduce Critical-Manifold Guided RRT (CMG-RRT), a sampling-based planner designed specifically for tight assembly problems. Our key observation is that in tight assemblies, valid solution paths lie on or near a critical manifold: the subset of configuration space consisting of poses with at least one contact point between parts. CMG-RRT guides exploration by adaptively biasing sampling toward neighborhoods of the critical manifold using a hierarchical subdivision of the configuration space. We prove that CMG-RRT is probabilistically complete under standard clearance assumptions. Empirical evaluation on challenging rotational assembly benchmarks demonstrates a 100% success rate across all tested instances, including, to the best of our knowledge, the first fully automatic solution of the Elk disentanglement puzzle. Our open source software is available through our project page: https://www.cgl.cs.tau.ac.il/projects/tight-assembly-planning.

</details>


### [99] [UMCP: A Unified Multi-Task Collaborative Perception Network for Luggage Trolley Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17950)
*Zhirui Sun,Zhihao Jiang,Yao Wang,Jianwei Peng,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的多任务协同感知网络（UMCP），基于YOLOv12架构，同时完成行李车检测、关键点检测和朝向估计，通过朝向特征增强模块（OFEM）和圆形概率分布建模（KL散度损失）提升朝向估计精度，在降低模型复杂度和计算成本的同时实现了 competitive 的整体精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉感知方法依赖级联多模型推理，导致推理延迟高、部署成本大，无法同时满足机器人自主行李车收集任务对高精度和实时性的双重要求。

Method: 提出UMCP统一多任务协同感知网络：基于YOLOv12架构，融合关键点特征与朝向特征，经过朝向特征增强模块（OFEM）进一步提升朝向估计精度，并采用圆形概率分布建模结合KL散度损失来优化朝向估计。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在取得competitive整体精度的同时，显著降低了模型复杂度和计算成本。

Conclusion: UMCP网络通过统一多任务协同学习，在降低部署成本和推理延迟的前提下，实现了高效准确的行李车检测、关键点检测和朝向估计，适用于机器人自主行李车收集任务。

Abstract: In robotic autonomous luggage trolley collection, robots must continuously localize scattered luggage trolleys in cluttered and dynamic environments. This requires the vision system to achieve both high accuracy and real-time performance. However, existing visual perception approaches for luggage trolleys often rely on cascaded multi-model inference, leading to increased inference latency and high deployment costs.
