To address these limitations, this article presents a unified multi-task collaborative perception network (UMCP) that simultaneously performs luggage trolley detection, keypoint detection and orientation estimation. Based on the YOLOv12 architecture, keypoint features are fused with orientation features and then fed into an orientation feature enhancement module (OFEM), thereby improving orientation estimation accuracy. In addition, circular probability distribution modeling with a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence loss is adopted to enhance orientation estimation accuracy further. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves competitive overall accuracy while substantially reducing model complexity and computational cost compared with existing methods. A website about this work is available at https://sites.google.com/view/robot-umcp.

</details>


### [100] [Does Robust VIO Need More Learning? Geometry-Verified Visual Measurements under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17956)
*Yangyang Ning,Shu Liang,Quanbo Ge,Tianchen Deng,Yuhua Qi,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种最小化学习的立体VIO框架，仅用SEA-RAFT学习稠密立体匹配和不确定性预测，其余模块（时间跟踪、几何验证、状态估计）保持显式，在分布偏移场景下比更深的端到端学习方法更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在分布偏移条件下，鲁棒VIO是否需要更深层的学习（如端到端运动/几何估计），还是仅将学习限制在视觉测量生成层面即可达到足够鲁棒性。

Method: 基于SEA-RAFT生成稠密立体对应及其不确定性，在稀疏特征位置采样稠密光流，利用预测不确定性和立体极线一致性进行过滤，并通过不确定性加权的重投影因子融入滑动窗口立体惯性估计器；将不确定性传播至立体三角化，用于各向异性3D高斯建图。

Result: 在EuRoC、VIODE和4Seasons数据集上，该方法在运动模糊、动态场景、光照变化及室内到室外大分布偏移下实现了准确稳定的估计；消融实验表明仅靠学习光流不够，增益来自结合学习对应、几何验证和不确定性加权。

Conclusion: 对于分布外鲁棒的VIO，精心集成的学习视觉测量比学习更大比例的估计流水线更有效。

Abstract: Learning is increasingly introduced into visual-inertial odometry (VIO), ranging from learned feature front-ends to learning-dominant motion and geometry estimation. However, learning more of the pipeline does not necessarily improve robustness when deployment conditions differ from the training distribution. This work asks whether robust VIO under distribution shift truly requires deeper learned estimation, or whether learning can be confined to visual measurement generation. We propose a minimal-learning stereo VIO framework in which SEA-RAFT is used only to propose dense stereo correspondences and predict their uncertainty, while temporal tracking, geometric verification, and state estimation remain explicit. Dense flow is sampled at sparse feature locations, filtered using predicted uncertainty and stereo epipolar consistency, and incorporated into a sliding-window stereo-inertial estimator through uncertainty-weighted reprojection factors. The same uncertainty is further propagated through stereo triangulation for downstream anisotropic 3D Gaussian mapping. Experiments on EuRoC, VIODE, and 4Seasons demonstrate accurate and stable estimation under motion blur, dynamic scenes, illumination changes, and large indoor-to-outdoor distribution shifts. Ablations show that learned flow alone is insufficient: the gains arise from combining learned correspondence proposals with geometric verification and uncertainty-aware weighting. These results suggest that, for OOD-robust VIO, carefully integrated learned visual measurements can be more effective than learning a larger fraction of the estimation pipeline. Code and configs for the benchmark will be open-source upon acceptance. A supplementary video is available at https://drive.google.com/file/d/1EVRhOkhanmNXHbQS1Vr80FoEIAYOYOV2/view

</details>


### [101] [MEVION: Low-Cost Open-Source Data Collection System for Powerful and High-Speed Dual-Arm Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17970)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Yoshiki Obinata,Hirokazu Ishida,Jihoon Oh,Temma Suzuki,Shintaro Inoue,Keita Yoneda,Ayumu Iwata,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MEVION是一种低成本、开源的双臂机器人数据采集系统，通过钣金焊接和四足机器人肘关节机构，在约14,000美元成本下实现高力/速度输出（每臂7.0kg，最大扭矩60Nm），支持重物操作和快速操控的模仿学习数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ALOHA等低成本开源双臂机器人数据采集系统在产生高力和高速度方面能力有限，难以处理重物或执行快速操作，亟需一种既保持低成本开源特性又能生成更大力和速度的双臂机器人系统。

Method: 通过大量采用钣金焊接构建大型机体结构，使用少量零件实现低成本制造并简化装配；肘关节采用类似四足机器人的闭链连杆机构，减少远端质量，提升末端执行器的力和速度输出；所有部件均可通过电商采购。

Result: MEVION配备四条6自由度带平行夹爪的机械臂，每臂重7.0kg，最大扭矩60Nm，整个系统构建成本约14,000美元，能够采集以往无法实现的目标操作任务数据，并支持基于模仿学习的运动生成。

Conclusion: MEVION成功实现了低成本、开源且具备高力/速度输出的双臂机器人数据采集系统，拓展了可采集操作任务的边界，为机器人基础模型研究提供了更强大的硬件平台。

Abstract: The global competition for developing robotic foundation models is intensifying. Among the data collection systems used for dual-arm robots, ALOHA is representative of being low-cost and open-source, and is widely adopted by researchers as a de facto standard. However, due to its limited ability to generate high forces and speeds, it is difficult to handle heavy objects or perform fast manipulations. To address this, we developed MEVION, a low-cost and open-source dual-arm robot data collection system capable of generating greater force and speed. All parts of this robot can be sourced through e-commerce, and by extensively utilizing sheet metal welding, its large body structure is constructed with a small number of components at low cost, while also simplifying assembly. MEVION is equipped with four 6-DoF arms with parallel grippers. Each arm weighs 7.0 kg and has a maximum torque of 60 Nm, and the entire system can be constructed for about USD 14,000. The elbow joint adopts a closed-link mechanism similar to those used in quadruped robots, which reduces the distal mass and enables higher force and speed output at the end-effector. We demonstrate that MEVION enables data collection for object manipulation tasks not previously possible and supports imitation learning-based motion generation. All hardware and software of this work are included in the Supplementary Materials or https://github.com/haraduka/mevion.

</details>


### [102] [RynnBrain 1.1: Towards More Capable and Generalizable Embodied Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17977)
*Kehan Li,Bohan Hou,Minghao Zhu,Tianyi Zhang,Zesen Cheng,Zhikai Wang,Sicong Leng,Xin Li,Xiao Lin,Biying Yao,Minghua Zeng,Jiangpin Liu,Ronghao Dang,Jiayan Guo,Siteng Huang,Haoyu Zhao,Heng Ping,Yaxi Zhao,Kexiang Wang,Tong Lu,Shengke Xue,Jiahao Tang,Yulei Wang,Zejing Wang,Jianwei Gao,Shijian Lu,Chengju Liu,Jianfei Yang,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnBrain 1.1 是一个涵盖2B、9B和122B-A10B三种规模的具身基础模型家族，通过统一的时空与物理基础框架训练，支持具身感知、空间推理、定位和规划。相比1.0版本，新增了接触点预测和原生3D定位能力，并开发了统一跨具身动作空间的VLA模型，在多个基准测试和真实机器人实验中取得了领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动具身基础模型在感知、空间推理与物理交互方面的对齐，弥补前代模型在接触点预测、3D定位及跨具身泛化方面的不足。

Method: 采用统一的时空与物理基础框架进行训练；引入接触点预测和原生3D定位（2B和9B模型）；构建统一跨具身动作空间与具身特定掩码的VLA模型。

Result: 122B-A10B模型在VSI-Bench、MMSI和RefSpatial-Bench上超越所有评估的闭源和开源模型；真实机器人实验中，RynnBrain初始化的策略优于Qwen基线和通用VLA；联合多任务多具身训练提升了过程分数和成功率。

Conclusion: RynnBrain 1.1在具身认知、定位和3D定位方面表现优异，验证了统一时空-物理框架、接触点预测及跨具身VLA训练范式的有效性。

Abstract: We present RynnBrain 1.1, a family of embodied foundation models spanning 2B, 9B, and 122B-A10B scales. Trained with a unified spatio-temporal and physically grounded framework, RynnBrain 1.1 supports embodied perception, spatial reasoning, localization, and planning. Compared with RynnBrain 1.0, it further introduces contact-point prediction across the model family and native 3D grounding for the 2B and 9B models, yielding representations and outputs that are more directly aligned with robot manipulation. We also develop RynnBrain-VLA with a unified cross-embodiment action space and embodiment-specific masking, and deploy it on Unitree G1, Astribot-S1, and Tianji-Wuji. RynnBrain 1.1 achieves strong results on embodied cognition, localization, and 3D grounding, with the 122B-A10B model outperforming all evaluated proprietary and open-source models on VSI-Bench, MMSI, and RefSpatial-Bench. Real-robot experiments show that RynnBrain-initialized policies outperform Qwen-based and representative generalist VLAs, while joint multi-task and multi-embodiment training improves process scores and success rates over per-task training.

</details>


### [103] [Distilling Global Traversability Priors for Image-based Affordance Prediction in Off-road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17984)
*Matthew Sivaprakasam,Samuel Triest,Micah Nye,Deegan Atha,Shehryar Khattak,David Fan,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从第一人称视角图像中提取可通行前沿的方法，利用卫星图像监督训练网络，以解决传统自主导航方法在长距离非结构化地形中的短视问题，在离线基准测试中提升超10%，并减少实际实验中的干预次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自主导航方法在长距离场景中存在短视行为，受限于深度传感器有效范围，丢弃超出感知距离的数据导致次优决策。现有方法无法有效利用远距离信息进行路径规划。

Method: 从第一人称视角图像中提取长距离可通过性感知前沿；利用卫星图像计算可行导航路径，构建图像/姿态配对数据集；通过监督学习训练网络，无需大量人工示范数据。

Result: 在多项离线基准测试中，性能较现有方法提升超过10%；在实际实验中减少人为干预次数。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过融合卫星图像先验知识与第一人称视角图像，有效恢复了被传统方法丢弃的远距离导航信息，显著提升了长距离越野自主导航性能。

Abstract: Standard methods for autonomous navigation in unstructured terrain are prone to myopic behaviors in long-horizon scenarios. The use of metric maps built from LiDAR or cameras provides necessary local geometry and semantic information but is strictly limited by depth sensing range. By discarding data beyond the mapping horizon robots suffer from suboptimal, short-sighted decisions. To recover this lost information, we focus on extracting long-range traversability-aware frontiers directly from first-person-view (FPV) images. By leveraging satellite imagery, we compute the set of feasible navigation paths for a dataset of image/pose pairs and use them to supervise our network, reducing the need for extensive human demonstration data. We demonstrate that this approach improves performance in long-range off-road navigation over existing methods by more than 10% in various offline benchmarks and reduces the number of human interventions incurred in a set of real-world experiments. More details can be found at https://theairlab.org/ss_frontiers_iros .

</details>


### [104] [Closing the Loop in Humanoid VLA: Persistent 3D Object Tokens for Verifiable Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18016)
*Peng Ren,Haoyang Ge,Jiang Zhao,Cong Huang,Yukun Shi,Pei Chi,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出持久对象令牌化（POT）方法，通过维护索引化3D对象记录并将其转化为对象令牌，构建POT-VLA系统实现可验证的人形机器人长时程定位操控。在Unitree G1上，POT-VLA将成功率从39/80提升至71/80，在外部参考任务上达到44/50 vs 37/50，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作策略在处理长时程人形机器人定位操控任务时，忽略了任务对象作为持久物理实体的特性（跨运动、接触、遮挡和恢复）。对象状态发散问题导致用于条件化全身动作的对象状态与用于判断动作是否实现预期物理关系的状态不一致，制约了闭环执行的可验证性。

Method: 提出持久对象令牌化（POT），从RGB-D观测中维护角色索引化的3D对象记录，并将其转换为对象令牌输入全身动作专家。构建POT-VLA系统，同一对象记录同时条件化动作生成并支持几何谓词检查，形成对象状态既可行动又可验证的闭环执行系统。

Result: 在Unitree G1上，POT-VLA将匹配的GR00T-N1.7基线从39/80提升至71/80（8个真实世界任务族）。在外部Being-0对齐参考任务上，POT-VLA达到44/50成功，对比Being-0论文报告的37/50。最大提升出现在需要维持3D关系的任务上。

Conclusion: 持久对象中心化状态是一种可验证的人形机器人VLA执行的有效抽象，特别是在需要维持3D空间关系的长时程任务中表现突出。POT方法通过弥合动作条件化与效果验证之间的对象状态鸿沟，显著提升了人形机器人定位操控的可靠性和可验证性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies are a promising foundation for general robot control, but long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires the robot to treat task objects as persistent physical entities across movement, contact, occlusion, and recovery. We study this problem as object-state divergence: the object state used to condition a whole-body action can differ from the state used to decide whether the action achieved the intended physical relation. We propose \emph{Persistent Object Tokenization} (POT), which maintains role-indexed 3D object records from RGB-D observations and converts them into object tokens for a whole-body action expert. Instantiated as \emph{POT-VLA}, the same object records condition action generation and support geometric predicate checks, yielding a closed-loop execution system in which object state is both actionable and verifiable. On a Unitree G1, POT-VLA improves a matched direct GR00T-N1.7 baseline from 39/80 to 71/80 successes over eight real-world task families. In an external Being-0-aligned reference, POT-VLA achieves 44/50 successes on aligned service tasks, compared with the 37/50 success reported by the Being-0 paper. The largest gains occur on tasks requiring maintained 3D relations, suggesting that persistent object-centered state is a useful abstraction for verifiable humanoid VLA execution.

</details>


### [105] [RoboHarness: Memory-Driven Orchestration of Heterogeneous Robot Policies for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18060)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Zhanguang Zhang,Mark Coates,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboHarness是一个统一框架，通过多模态执行记忆和Memory Bridge机制，实现异构机器人策略（VLA、RL、TAMP等）的能力感知分解、路由和无重训练的策略切换，在长时域任务中显著提升零样本规划和分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划方法通常假设同质化、预定义技能，忽略了异构策略间不确定的能力边界和跨策略分布不匹配问题，无法有效编排具有互补优势的多样化策略来完成长时域机器人任务。

Method: 提出RoboHarness框架：(1) 将独立开发的机器人控制系统封装为可复用的智体技能；(2) 利用多模态执行记忆和在线证据表征策略能力边界，实现能力感知的任务分解与路由；(3) 设计Memory Bridge机制，检索下一策略的执行轨迹，估计其分布内状态区域，引导机器人进入该区域，无需联合策略重训练。

Result: 在三个公开基准、500个定制化任务和135次真实机器人实验中，RoboHarness实现了有效的能力感知路由和稳定的策略编排，在零样本长时域规划和分布外鲁棒性上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: RoboHarness作为一种通用框架，能够兼容多种异构机器人策略（含VLA、RL、TAMP等），通过能力感知分解和Memory Bridge策略切换机制，有效解决长时域任务中的策略编排挑战。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks require diverse capabilities that no single policy can reliably provide. Heterogeneous policies offer complementary strengths, but orchestrating them requires reasoning over uncertain capability boundaries and cross-policy distribution mismatch, which are largely overlooked by existing planning methods built on homogeneous, predefined skills with fixed applicability. We propose RoboHarness, a unified framework that encapsulates independently developed robot control systems as reusable agentic skills. Although instantiated in this work with VLAs, RL policies, and task-and-motion planning (TAMP) systems, RoboHarness is designed as a general framework compatible with a broader range of robot policies, such as navigation policies, model predictive controllers, and world-action models. RoboHarness uses multi-modal execution memory and online evidence to characterize policy capability boundaries for capability-aware decomposition and routing. To stabilize policy handoffs, its Memory Bridge retrieves execution trajectories associated with the next policy, estimates its in-distribution state region, and guides the robot toward that region without joint policy retraining. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks, 500 customized tasks, and 135 real-robot experiments demonstrate effective capability-aware routing and stable policy orchestration, yielding substantial improvements in zero-shot long-horizon planning and out-of-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [106] [UniETP: Unifying Environments for Generalizable Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18062)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Ziyou Wang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniETP，一个统一了四个具身任务规划模拟器（AI2-THOR、VirtualHome、Habitat、BEHAVIOR）的接口，通过标准化观测与动作空间、构建多样化难度数据集，解决了现有模拟器碎片化问题，并基于该基准评估了当前模型的具身规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身任务规划研究中，模拟器和数据集各自独立，采用不同的观测格式、动作类型和任务领域，导致模型综合评估困难且训练数据可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出UniETP统一接口：1）将四个模拟器标准化为一致的观测和动作空间，并构建支持复杂任务目标的评估系统；2）在任务逻辑、实例基元、指令理解等维度增强任务多样性和复杂度，自动构建多难度层级的数据集。

Result: 基于所提基准进行了大量实验，评估了当前模型的具身规划能力并分析了性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: UniETP通过标准化与多样性并重的设计，为通用具身规划研究提供了统一的评测平台和多样化训练数据。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the problem of Embodied Task Planning, where an agent is required to execute a sequence of atomic actions within an interactive environment to complete a user-specified task. Though a variety of simulators and datasets have previously been built for this task, these efforts are largely isolated, with each using its own observation format, action type, and task domain. This fragmentation complicates comprehensive model evaluation and hinders the scalability of training data. As an effort towards generalizable embodied planning, we propose UniETP, a unified interface integrating four commonly-used simulators (AI2-THOR, VirtualHome, Habitat, BEHAVIOR). UniETP is characterized by both standardization and diversity. On one hand, it formalizes all the simulators into a consistent observation and action space, and builds an evaluation system to support complicated task goal. On the other hand, it enhances task diversity and complexity across dimensions like task logic, instance grounding, and instruction understanding, constructing a new dataset with varied levels of difficulty in an automatic manner. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark are conducted to evaluate the embodied planning capabilities of recent models and analyze the performance bottlenecks. Codes and data will be available at https://github.com/woyut/UniETP .

</details>


### [107] [Technical Design Review of Duke Robotics Club's Oogway & Crush: AUVs for RoboSub 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18075)
*Patrick Zheng,Saagar Arya,Hung Le,Mathew Chu,Nathanael Ren,Niko Weaver,Isabella Chen,Jill Wang,Raine Cheng,Siddharth Kini,Avrick Altmann,Srinath Iyer,Ivan Chen,Ian Suh,Parker Jones,Pierson Jones,Sebastian deSouza,Suhaani Sriram,Suvas Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 杜克机器人俱乐部介绍了其用于RoboSub 2026的AUV“Oogway”和“Crush”，通过机械、电气和软件三方面的可靠性投资，首次同时针对移动、视觉、操作和声学跟踪四个设计目标，实现了更广泛的任务覆盖与高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往自主水下航行器（AUV）的任务范围较为狭窄，作者希望首次同时挑战RoboSub的全部四个设计目标（移动、视觉、操作、声学跟踪），以提升竞赛竞争力和系统综合能力。

Method: 机械方面：新增两个推进器并采用CFD优化外壳以增强俯仰稳定性；电气方面：修复不可靠连接点并升级推进器控制硬件，重新设计声学系统并增加高阶滤波器定制PCB以提高水听器检测可靠性；软件方面：改进状态估计、声纳目标检测、视觉驱动任务规划和IVC以实现更协调的自主运行。同时投资测试基础设施以最大化有限的测试时间。

Result: 实现了首次同时尝试所有四个设计目标，并且在维持竞赛所需可靠性的前提下，能够执行更广泛的任务集。

Conclusion: 通过对机械、电气和软件三个子系统的持续可靠性投资，并结合测试基础设施的优化，可以在扩大任务范围的同时保持高可靠性，从而满足竞赛策略需求。

Abstract: The Duke Robotics Club presents Oogway and Crush, our AUVs for RoboSub 2026. This year's strategy expands on our previously narrowed scope, targeting all four of RoboSub's design goals for the first time: movement, vision, manipulation, and acoustic tracking. This expansion is based on sustained reliability investment across all three subsystems. Mechanically, Crush gained two additional thrusters and a CFD-optimized case, providing pitch stability. Electrically, we addressed accumulated failure points by repairing unreliable connections and upgraded thruster control hardware. We also redesigned our acoustics system, adding a new custom PCB with higher-order filters, significantly improving pinger detection reliability. On the software side, improvements to state estimation, sonar-based object detection, vision-driven task planning, and IVC enable more capable and coordinated autonomous runs. Paired with investments in our testing infrastructure to maximize our limited pool time, we can now attempt a broader set of tasks while maintaining the reliability our competition strategy demands.

</details>


### [108] [Importance Sampling and PCA for Finding Failures in Commercial Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18106)
*Hailey Warner,Duncan Eddy,Shreya Parjan,Caroline Cahilly,Harrison Delecki,Matthias Kleinstauber,Chaitanya Shinde,Jerry Lopez,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次将两种稀有故障发现算法（AST和DiFS）应用于商业级自动驾驶卡车系统，在并道和切入场景中成功发现模拟碰撞，并引入PCA统计分析对故障进行分类和诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往稀有故障发现方法仅在简单学术驾驶堆栈的仿真中验证，未能证明其能否推广至商业系统使用的更鲁棒规划器，存在方法泛化性的空白。

Method: 1) 应用AST（基于强化学习搜索最可能导致碰撞的噪声轨迹）和DiFS（基于去噪扩散模型采样多样化故障）；2) 引入PCA统计分析方法对故障进行分类、识别关键时间步；3) 对主成分聚类并逆转PCA变换以恢复通用噪声轨迹。

Result: 两种算法均在传统蒙特卡洛仿真无法发现的场景中成功发现模拟碰撞；恢复的通用噪声轨迹能在相同和相似场景中复现故障。

Conclusion: 该方法建立了从故障发现到感知层面缺陷系统性诊断的路径，证明了稀有故障发现算法可推广至商业级自动驾驶系统。

Abstract: Methods for discovering rare failures in autonomous systems have so far been demonstrated almost exclusively in simulations with simple, academic driving stacks, leaving open whether they generalize to the more robust planners used in commercial systems. We address this gap by applying two rare-event discovery algorithms to a commercial autonomous trucking stack. Adaptive stress testing (AST) uses reinforcement learning to search for the most likely noise trajectories leading to a simulated collision, while diffusion-based failure sampling (DiFS) trains a denoising diffusion model to sample a diverse set of failures. We show that both algorithms find simulated collisions during merge and cut-in maneuvers where traditional Monte Carlo simulation does not. To make these failures actionable, we introduce a statistical analysis based on principal component analysis (PCA) that classifies failures into common modes and identifies the timesteps that most influence the outcome. We cluster the principal components and invert the PCA transform to recover generalized noise trajectories, and show that these trajectories reproduce failures in identical and similar scenarios. This provides a path from failure discovery to systematic diagnosis of perception-level flaws.

</details>


### [109] [Isaac Sim-to-Real: Reinforcement Learning based Locomotion for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18135)
*Jordan Dowdy,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的鲁棒运动框架，利用Nvidia Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab进行训练，实现了四足机器人Unitree Go1的零样本仿真到现实迁移，在物理硬件上验证了与集成控制器相当的线速度跟踪性能，更强的抗干扰能力，以及2.0 m/s线速度和1.8 rad/s角速度的运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习策略在仿真中表现优异但部署到物理系统时面临的仿真到现实（sim-to-real）差距问题，实现无需额外调参的零样本迁移。

Method: 提出一种基于强化学习的鲁棒运动框架，使用Nvidia Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab作为训练工具，在仿真环境中训练策略后直接零样本部署到Unitree Go1四足机器人物理硬件上。

Result: 在物理硬件上验证了与集成控制器相当的线速度跟踪性能，具备更强的抗大扰动恢复能力，实现了2.0 m/s的线速度和1.8 rad/s的角速度。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现零样本仿真到现实的策略迁移，在四足机器人上获得鲁棒的全身控制性能，且运动能力优于或等同于现有集成控制器。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to locomotion have risen in popularity in recent years, showing the capability for complex legged locomotion and whole-body control. Reinforcement learning (RL), the primary learning-based approach for locomotion, often utilizes a high-performance simulation tool, providing a controlled and efficient training and development environment. However, policies that perform well in simulation frequently encounter unexpected challenges when deployed on a physical system, known as the sim-to-real gap. This work presents a robust RL locomotion framework capable of whole-body control. The proposed RL framework utilizes Nvidia's new set of simulation tools, Isaac Sim, and its companion RL framework, Isaac Lab, for training, achieving a zero-shot sim-to-real policy. The performance of our policy is validated on physical hardware using the Unitree Go1, with experimental results showing similar velocity tracking performance to the quadruped's integrated controller, with a greater ability to recover from large disturbances, and achieve linear velocities of 2.0 m/s and angular velocities of 1.8 rad/s.

</details>


### [110] [World Translation: Minimizing Sim-to-Real Gap with Backward Dynamics Extraction and Unpaired Domain Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18154)
*Xinchen Yao,Leixin Chang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出World Translation方法，通过从已观测的状态转移中逆向提取不可观测的动力学信息，并将其作为无配对域翻译问题在仿真与现实之间传递，从而解决部分可观测性导致的动力学建模不准确问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的机器人策略部署到现实世界时存在sim-to-real gap，现有基于学习的动力学模型面临部分可观测性问题：相同观测可能因不可观测因素分支到不同状态转移，且当观测历史无信息量时（如突发接触事件）现有方法会失效。

Method: 提出World Translation方法：(1) 从已观测的转移中逆向提取不可观测的动力学信息，而非从历史向前预测；(2) 将跨仿真与现实的动力学建模转化为无配对域翻译问题，保留动力学内容同时转移域风格；(3) 结合仿真器的确定性但物理不完美特性与学习模型的准确性但欠定特性。

Result: 在类人、四足和机械臂平台上，该方法在动力学建模精度上优于基线方法，当不可观测因素无法从观测历史中恢复时增益最大。在Go2四足机器人上的真实部署验证了策略迁移的改进。

Conclusion: World Translation通过逆向提取不可观测动力学信息并采用域翻译框架，有效解决了部分可观测性下的动力学建模问题，提升了sim-to-real迁移效果。

Abstract: The gap between simulation and reality remains a fundamental challenge in deploying simulation-trained robotic policies in the real world. Real-to-sim methods narrow this gap from the real side, learning transition dynamics from real data to build a more realistic digital world. Learned dynamics models are their dominant instance. Such methods, however, face a partial observability problem: the same observation may branch to different transitions due to unobservable factors. Existing methods assume these factors can be recovered from observation history. However, this may fail whenever observation history is uninformative, such as a sudden contact event with no prior warning. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{World Translation}, which exploits a complementary strength of simulators and learned dynamics. Simulators are deterministic but physically imperfect, while learned models are accurate but underdetermined under partial observability. Rather than predicting transitions forward from history, we extract the unobservable dynamics information backward from an observed transition, then translate this feature across simulation and reality as an unpaired domain-translation problem that preserves dynamics content while transferring domain style. Experiments across humanoid, quadruped, and manipulator platforms show that our method achieves more accurate dynamics modeling than baselines, with the largest gains when unobservable factors cannot be recovered from observation history. Real-robot deployment on Go2 quadruped confirms improved policy transfer.

</details>


### [111] [Imitation of Arm Gestures by the Semi-Humanoid Robot NICO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18197)
*Anastasiya Ihnatovich,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于MediaPipe姿态估计和解析几何方法，开发了半人形机器人NICO对人类手臂手势的模仿系统，仅通过单目RGB输入即可生成有意义的模仿动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无缝人机交互需要机器人具备模仿人类手势的能力，而人形机器人因其拟人特征在此方面具有优势。

Method: 使用MediaPipe框架从RGB帧中获取人体3D关键点坐标（包括手臂关节和手部关键点），通过解析几何关系计算关节角度，然后将角度映射到NICO机器人的电机配置并执行预定义运动序列。

Result: 对六名不同身高参与者的多种代表性手臂手势进行初步实验，表明该方法可从单目RGB输入生成有意义的模仿动作，但在复杂姿态和腕部相关动作方面存在局限性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于解析几何和MediaPipe的方法能够实现半人形机器人对手臂手势的模仿，但复杂姿态和手腕运动的精度有待改进。

Abstract: Seamless human-robot interaction (HRI) requires a number of perceptual and motor abilities from the robot, one of them being the imitation of human gestures. Humanoid robots have an advantage in HRI thanks to their anthropomorphic features. In this work, we develop a system for imitation of human arm gestures by the semi-humanoid robot NICO based on analytical geometry and a pretrained MediaPipe pose-estimation model. For each input RGB frame, 3D coordinates of relevant human body landmarks, including arm joints and hand keypoints, are obtained using the MediaPipe framework. Joint angles are then computed from these coordinates using derived geometric relations. Finally, the computed angles are properly mapped to NICO's motor configuration and executed in a predefined motion sequence. Preliminary experiments on several representative arm gestures with six participants of different height indicate that the proposed method can produce meaningful imitative motions from monocular RGB input only, while also highlighting limitations in more complex poses and wrist-related movements.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Adaptive Safety Margins for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18200)
*Junyi Hu,Shuaihang Yuan,Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala,Anthony Tzes,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种上下文条件安全评价器(context-conditioned safety critic)，用于自适应的扩散轨迹排序，解决移动机器人在杂乱室内场景中因固定安全裕度误校准导致的导航失败问题。该方法将安全偏好分解为三项：安全项（含清障预算惩罚与控制障碍函数残差）、效率项（含平滑度惩罚与安全门控绕路比惩罚）、距离约束匹配项（防止裕度坍塌）。通过特权ESDF仿真训练并经两阶段师生蒸馏到感知选择器，在HM3D/MP3D点目标导航任务中取得了最高SR和SPL，并零样本迁移至Unitree G1人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的轨迹规划器虽然能生成多样化的候选轨迹，但可靠的选择机制仍是瓶颈，因为固定安全裕度在不同场景下表现不稳定：保守裕度导致绕路和超时，激进裕度在感知偏差下产生近边界捷径。需要一种能根据上下文自适应调整清障偏好的评价器。

Method: 提出上下文条件安全评价器，将扩散轨迹排序的偏好分解为三项：1) 安全项：包含清障预算惩罚(waypoint-wise)和控制障碍函数残差(transition-wise)；2) 效率项：结合平滑度惩罚和安全门控绕路比惩罚（避免绕路但惩罚危险捷径）；3) 距离约束匹配项：将学习到的清障预算锚定到实际ESDF距离，防止裕度坍塌。训练时使用特权ESDF几何信息，再通过两阶段师生蒸馏将知识迁移至仅使用RGB-D感知的选择器。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D数据集（含跨数据集迁移）的PointGoal导航任务中，该方法在成功率和SPL指标上均超越扩散基线、优化基线和强化学习基线。在仿真中训练后，零样本迁移到Unitree G1人形机器人，无需任务特定微调即可在杂乱室内场景中导航。

Conclusion: 自适应上下文安全评价器有效解决了扩散规划器的轨迹选择瓶颈，通过可分解的安全-效率-距离约束偏好实现了鲁棒的清障行为，且感知仅依赖RGB-D，无需在线ESDF计算，具备良好的跨场景和跨平台迁移能力。

Abstract: Robots in cluttered indoor spaces often fail not because they cannot generate collision-free paths, but because a fixed safety margin is mis-calibrated: conservative margins cause detours and timeouts, while permissive margins lead to near-boundary shortcuts under perception bias. Diffusion-based planners propose diverse trajectory candidates from egocentric RGB-D, yet reliable selection remains the bottleneck. We propose a context-conditioned safety critic that learns an adaptive clearance preference for ranking diffusion proposals, decomposed into three complementary terms: (i) a safety term with a clearance-budget penalty and a control-barrier-function residual for waypoint- and transition-wise safety, (ii) an efficiency term combining a smoothness penalty with a safety-gated detour-ratio penalty that avoids detours without incentivizing risky shortcuts, and (iii) a distance-constraint matching term that anchors the learned budget to realized ESDF clearances to prevent margin collapse. We train the critic with privileged ESDF geometry in simulation and distill it into a perception-only selector via a two-stage teacher-student procedure. On PointGoal navigation in HM3D and MP3D, including cross-dataset transfer, our method achieves the highest success rate (SR) and success weighted by path length (SPL) among strong diffusion, optimization, and RL baselines. Trained purely in simulation, it transfers to a Unitree G1 humanoid and navigates cluttered indoor scenes without task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [113] [Optimization of sim-to-real transfer in the humanoid robot NICO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18210)
*Juraj Gavura,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于低代价触觉标定的抓取流水线，通过结合YOLO目标检测、立体视觉定位和视觉反馈模型，在仅使用机器人内置低分辨率鱼眼相机的情况下，实现了从仿真到物理机器人的桌面物体抓取，有效弥合了sim-to-real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取中仿真到现实的差距会导致定位误差，影响抓取成功率。作者旨在解决这一sim-to-real问题，提出一种无需RGB-D相机、动作捕捉或外部跟踪系统的低成本标定方法。

Method: 在前期2D触觉标定工作基础上，扩展至桌面抓取：(1) YOLO-based物体与手部检测；(2) 基于立体视觉的定位（利用机器人内置低分辨率鱼眼相机）；(3) 抓取执行的任务特定校正。同时实现了视觉反馈模型，在抓取前将机器手与检测到的物体对齐。

Result: 完全非线性标定模型在校准区域内表现最佳，而视觉反馈模型在整个桌面工作空间上实现了最高的整体抓取成功率。

Conclusion: 所提出的标定流水线能够有效缩小sim-to-real差距，实现低成本、无外部设备依赖的桌面物体抓取，其中视觉反馈模型在全工作空间泛化性更好。

Abstract: Robotic grasping requires accurate coordination between visual perception, object localization, inverse kinematics, and hand control. However, when movements planned in simulation are executed on a physical robot, the sim-to-real gap can cause small positioning errors that prevent successful grasping. In our previous work, we introduced a low-cost haptic calibration method that improved 2D reaching accuracy of the humanoid robot NICO. In this paper, we extend this approach from reaching to tabletop object grasping by adding YOLO-based object and hand detection, stereo vision-based localization using the robot's built-in low-resolution fisheye cameras, and task-specific corrections for grasp execution. Together, these components form a novel calibration-based grasping pipeline that does not require RGB-D cameras, motion capture, or external tracking systems. We also implemented a visual feedback model that aligns the robot hand with the detected object before grasping. Our results show that the fully nonlinear calibration model achieved the best performance inside the calibrated area, while the visual feedback model achieved the highest overall grasping success across the full tabletop workspace.

</details>


### [114] [FM-VLA: Force-based Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18231)
*Ruicheng Li,Qixiu Li,Ruichun Ma,Yu Deng,Lin Luo,Zhiying Du,Jianfeng Xiang,Huizhi Liang,Ruicheng Wang,Jiaolong Yang,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FM-VLA，一种带有力记忆的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过变分自编码器将力历史编码为紧凑的力记忆令牌，使VLA模型能够推理非马尔可夫、接触丰富的操作任务中的时序上下文，在三个记忆依赖任务上取得超80%成功率且推理开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA模型依赖视觉记忆（采样历史图像帧），但视觉方法在时序事件视觉模糊时（如多次按钮按压动作微小）计算昂贵且存在根本局限，因此需要引入力记忆来感知接触事件历史。

Method: 1) 使用VAE预训练力时间序列重构，将力历史编码为紧凑的力记忆令牌；2) 将力潜在表示和短状态历史作为额外的条件令牌注入动作专家模块；3) 使VLA模型能够利用累积的接触事件历史来指导操作。

Result: 在寻找隐藏块、按压按钮、擦盘子三个记忆依赖任务上，FM-VLA以极小推理开销取得超80%成功率，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 力记忆是一种轻量级、高效且有效的替代视觉记忆的方案，能够处理视觉上模糊的时序事件推理，为机器人操作中的非马尔可夫接触丰富任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved impressive generalization in robotic manipulation, and recent memory-augmented VLAs have relaxed the Markovian assumption by conditioning on past images or language summaries. Vision-based memory approaches address this by conditioning on sampled past image frames, but they are computationally expensive and fundamentally limited when temporal events are visually ambiguous, e.g., pushing a button multiple times with small movements. We propose FM-VLA, a VLA model with force-based memory, enabling temporal context reasoning for non-Markovian, contact-rich manipulation. We encode force histories into compact force memory tokens with a variational autoencoder (VAE) pretrained with force time series reconstruction. By projecting force latent representations and short state history as additional conditioning tokens to the action expert module, we enable VLAs to leverage accumulated contact event history to guide manipulation. We evaluate FM-VLA on three memory-dependent tasks, including finding a hidden block, pressing a button, and wiping a dish for a specific number of times. Our lightweight force memory achieves over 80% success rate with minimal inference overhead, significantly outperforming baseline approaches. Project page: https://qft-333.github.io/FM-VLA-Page/

</details>


### [115] [Patch Policy: Efficient Embodied Control via Dense Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18236)
*Gaoyue Zhou,Zichen Jeff Cui,Ada Langford,Bowen Tan,Yann LeCun,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Patch Policy 是一种轻量级的架构扩展，通过块因果注意力掩码使基于Transformer的策略直接利用预训练的密集ViT块特征，无需完整的视觉-语言模型。在模拟和真实环境套件中，相比全局池化策略提升40%，相比OpenVLA-OFT提升18%，仅使用其0.7%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的机器人策略要么将每个观察压缩为单个全局token（损失空间细节），要么从头训练视觉骨干（损失大规模预训练优势）。大视觉-语言-动作模型(VLA)虽使用密集块特征但过于沉重缓慢。因此需要一种轻量级方法，在保留密集空间信息的同时避免VLM的计算开销。

Method: 核心是块因果注意力掩码(block-causal attention mask)，在保持标准策略时间因果性的同时，允许模型关注每个观察中的多个块token及其他状态信息。这是一个极小的架构扩展，使基于Transformer的策略能直接消耗预训练的密集ViT块特征。

Result: 在4个模拟和3个真实环境套件中，相比使用最先进全局池化表示的策略，相对性能提升40%。超越微调后的OpenVLA-OFT 18%，同时仅使用其约0.7%的参数。模型轻量、快速且高效。

Conclusion: Patch Policy 为机器人社区提供了一条利用视觉表示学习持续进步的管道，无需牺牲高频反应控制所需的训练效率或推理速度。

Abstract: Pretrained dense visual features from Vision Transformers (ViTs) are powerful yet have been underutilized in robot learning. Modern robot policies either compress each observation into a single global token, or rely on visual backbones trained from scratch, sacrificing both fine-grained spatial detail and the benefits of large-scale visual pre-training. While there exist policies that do operate on dense patch features like large vision-language-action models (VLAs), they tend to be heavy and slow, inheriting the full cost of a billion-parameter vision-language model (VLM) backbone. We close this gap with Patch Policy, a minimal architectural extension that enables transformer-based policies to consume dense pre-trained patch tokens directly without the computational overhead of a full VLM. At its core is a block-causal attention mask that preserves the temporal causality of standard policies while letting the model attend over many patch tokens per observation, alongside other state information. Patch Policy is lightweight, fast, and highly effective. Across four simulated and three real-world environment suites, our method achieves a 40% relative improvement over policies using state-of-the-art global-pooled representations. Furthermore, it surpasses fine-tuned OpenVLA-OFT by 18% while using roughly 0.7% of the parameters. We believe Patch Policy provides a pipeline for the robotics community to readily leverage continuing progress in visual representation learning, without sacrificing the training efficiency or inference speed required for high-frequency, reactive control. Videos can be viewed at https://patch-policy.github.io

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [An Interactive Interface for Control Integration in Mid-Fidelity Wind Farm Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16386)
*Zekai Chen,Casey Heiskell,Ryozo Nagamune*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: ffconnect 是一个用于 FAST.Farm 的开源 Python 接口，支持风电场控制的交互式设计与验证，具有低运行时开销和丰富的 API 功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电场控制需要通过数据驱动和人工智能技术实现更智能的系统，但缺乏支持交互式设计与验证的仿真工具。

Method: 开发了 ffconnect，一个基于 Python 的通用开源接口，重构了 FAST.Farm 的 API，提供更丰富的状态访问，并与现代科学计算和机器学习生态系统集成。

Result: 在不同仿真时长和风电场规模下，ffconnect 相比原始 FAST.Farm 的运行时开销可忽略不计，并通过偏航跟踪案例研究验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: ffconnect 作为一个开源工具，为风电场控制的交互式设计和验证提供了高效、易用的解决方案。

Abstract: Wind farm control (WFC) plays a crucial role in mitigating the wake effect, the negative aerodynamic interactions among wind turbines. Recent advances in data-driven control and artificial intelligence offer new opportunities to design more intelligent WFC systems, motivating the need for a tool that supports interactive design and validation in simulation. To address this, we present ffconnect, a general, open-source Python-based interface for FAST.Farm, a mid-fidelity wind farm simulator. Compared to prior work, ffconnect introduces a restructured Application Programming Interface (API) with enriched state access and supports integrating FAST.Farm with modern scientific computing and machine learning ecosystems by building entirely on Python. In experiments, ffconnect shows negligible runtime overhead compared to the original FAST.Farm across a range of simulation lengths and farm sizes, and demonstrates its effectiveness through a yaw-tracking case study. Finally, we provide the source code of ffconnect to keep it accessible for general users.

</details>


### [117] [A 0.74-mW K$_u$-band Cryogenic SiGe LNA in 130-nm BiCMOS with 14-K Noise Temperature for Scalable Quantum Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16505)
*Xiuhan Chen,Haoling Li,Gun Suer,Leonardo Ranzani,Kin Chung Fong,Aravind Nagulu,Najme Ebrahimi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款面向亚开尔文量子比特读出链路的亚毫瓦级K$_u$波段低温低噪声放大器（LNA），采用GF 130CBIC SiGe BiCMOS工艺实现。该四级级联LNA采用巴伦耦合级间匹配、2位开关控制的Q增强谐振腔进行频率调谐，以及压控交叉耦合负跨导单元。在2.5K温度下测试，LNA在12-18GHz频段内平均噪声温度约14K，16.5GHz处峰值增益28dB，功耗仅0.74mW，是已报道中K$_u$波段低温SiGe LNA中功耗最低的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为亚开尔文（sub-Kelvin）量子比特读出链路设计低功耗、低温低噪声放大器，该链路需要在Still级集成约瑟夫森参量放大器（JPA）和SiGe LNA，以满足量子计算中对极低噪声和极低功耗的需求。

Method: 采用GF 130CBIC SiGe BiCMOS工艺实现四级级联共源共栅（cascode）结构LNA，创新点包括：1）巴伦耦合的级间匹配；2）2位开关控制的Q增强谐振腔用于频率调谐；3）压控交叉耦合负跨导（negative-$g_m$）单元；4）针对不同温度级优化Q单元工作点，在稳定条件下实现最佳带内性能。

Result: 在2.5K低温下表征：12-18GHz频段内平均噪声温度约14K，16.5GHz处峰值增益28dB，功耗仅0.74mW。

Conclusion: 该LNA在K$_u$波段实现了已报道SiGe低温LNA中最低的直流功耗（0.74mW），适用于极低功耗约束的亚开尔文量子比特读出链路应用。

Abstract: This paper presents a sub-milliwatt K$_u$-band cryogenic low-noise amplifier (LNA) implemented in GlobalFoundries (GF) 130CBIC SiGe BiCMOS, intended for a sub-Kelvin qubit readout chain integrating a JPA and SiGe LNA on the Still stage. The four-stage cascode LNA features balun-coupled interstage matching with a Q-enhanced resonance tank controlled by 2-bit switches tank for frequency tuning and a voltage-controlled cross-coupled negative-$g_m$ cell. The operating point of the Q-cell is optimized at each temperature stage to achieve the best in-band performance under a stable condition. Characterized at 2.5 K for the initial demonstration, the LNA achieves an average noise temperature of $\sim$14 K across 12-18 GHz and 28 dB peak gain around 16.5 GHz, while consuming only 0.74 mW. To the authors' knowledge, this is the lowest reported DC power for a K$_u$-band cryogenic SiGe LNA.

</details>


### [118] [Distributed Adaptive Estimation of Unknown Nonlinear Systems without Input Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16510)
*Moh Kamalul Wafi,Milad Siami*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对离散时间非线性系统，在有向通信网络上提出了一种分布式自适应状态估计方法。每个感知智能体仅利用本地测量和邻居信息进行源状态估计，无需共享激励或控制输入。通过归一化自适应估计方案识别未知线性和非线性动态，并基于Lyapunov方法证明了估计误差动力学的输入-状态稳定性（ISS）。开发了显式范数基和LMI基的Schur稳定性条件来表征网络耦合效应，并通过数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式状态估计方法多针对已知动态系统或线性系统，对于未知动态的非线性系统在分布式场景下的自适应状态估计缺乏系统性的理论研究，尤其是在有向通信拓扑下如何保证估计的稳定性和收敛性。

Method: 1) 提出归一化自适应估计方案处理未知线性和非线性动态；2) 基于Lyapunov方法建立估计误差动力学的ISS稳定性分析；3) 开发显式范数基和LMI基的Schur稳定性条件，包括鲁棒公式以应对模型不确定性。

Result: 在星型、环型和路径型通信拓扑上的数值仿真验证了分布式状态估计的准确性；稳定性条件的有效性得到验证；计算结果表明估计器随网络规模扩展具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式自适应状态估计方法能够在有向通信网络上有效处理未知非线性动态，具备理论上的稳定性保证和实际中的可扩展性，为分布式传感网络中的未知源状态估计提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper studies distributed adaptive state estimation for discrete-time nonlinear systems with unknown source dynamics over directed communication networks. Each sensing agent estimates the source state using only local measurements and information exchanged with neighboring agents, enabling a fully distributed implementation without requiring shared excitation or control inputs. A normalized adaptive estimation scheme is proposed to identify unknown linear and nonlinear dynamics while ensuring robust discrete-time adaptation. A Lyapunov-based analysis establishes input-to-state stability (ISS) of the estimation error dynamics, guaranteeing bounded adaptive parameters under bounded disturbances and asymptotic convergence of the estimation errors in the disturbance-free case under suitable conditions. To characterize the network-induced coupling, explicit norm-based and LMI-based Schur stability conditions are developed for the coupling operator, including a robust formulation accounting for bounded model uncertainty. Numerical simulations on star, cyclic, and path communication topologies demonstrate accurate distributed state estimation and validate the proposed stability conditions. Computational results further show that the proposed estimator scales efficiently with the network size.

</details>


### [119] [A Unified Statistical Framework for Multicopter Propeller Damage Diagnosis Based on Functionally Pooled Models and Bayesian Quantification: Experimental Flight Test Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16512)
*Shinan Huang,Jingxi Zhu,Fotis Kopsaftopoulos*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机时间序列的框架，利用功能池化自回归（FP-AR）模型进行多旋翼无人机螺旋桨损伤诊断。该框架仅使用标准惯性测量单元数据，实现了损伤检测、电机级识别和损伤量级估计，并通过贝叶斯推断提供后验估计和不确定性表征。室外飞行实验验证了其在多种损伤程度下的跨飞行一致性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机螺旋桨损伤会导致飞行性能下降甚至坠毁，现有方法通常需要额外传感器或特定工况下的数据。本文旨在开发一种仅依赖标准IMU数据、无需重调参数且数据高效的损伤诊断方法。

Method: 采用FP-AR模型对IMU时间序列数据进行功能池化建模，通过残差统计检验进行损伤检测，通过模型选择实现电机级损伤识别，通过贝叶斯推断提供损伤量级的后验估计和不确定性量化。

Result: 在户外八字轨迹飞行实验中，六通道IMU数据（三轴加速度和角速度）的分析表明该方法具有跨飞行一致性，无需针对不同工况重新调参。贝叶斯方法相比传统批处理方法提供了更稳定的估计和显式的不确定性表征。

Conclusion: 该框架为多旋翼结构健康监测提供了一种数据高效、可解释且统计严谨的解决方案，在真实户外环境中验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this work, a stochastic time series-based framework is introduced for multicopter propeller damage diagnosis using functionally pooled autoregressive (FP-AR) models. The framework addresses damage detection, motor-level identification, and damage magnitude estimation using only standard inertial measurement unit data, without requiring additional sensors. Functional pooling provides a compact representation of system dynamics across varying operating conditions and supports reliable model estimation from short data records. Damage detection is performed through statistical testing of prediction residuals, damage identification through model selection, and damage quantification through a Bayesian inference scheme that provides posterior estimates and uncertainty bounds. The framework is experimentally evaluated through outdoor flight tests of a custom-built hexacopter following figure-eight trajectories under ambient wind disturbances. Six IMU channels, including three-axis acceleration and angular velocity, are analyzed across multiple motors and propeller damage levels. The results demonstrate consistent cross-flight performance without case-specific retuning. Compared with conventional batch-based quantification, the Bayesian approach provides more stable estimates and explicit uncertainty characterization. Overall, the proposed framework offers a data-efficient, interpretable, and statistically rigorous solution for multicopter structural health monitoring.

</details>


### [120] [Comparative Analysis of Linepack Impact in Hydrogen and Natural Gas Networks under Dynamic Operating Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16527)
*Amin Salehi,Janne Seppanen,Mahdi Pourakbari-Kasmaei*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了在压缩机故障条件下，氢气（H2）和天然气（CH4）管网中管线存气（linepack）的动态影响。通过三天动态模拟发现：H2的瞬态恢复速度更快但管线存气量更少；相同管径下H2压降更小、负荷削减更少，但减小H2管径后其压降增大、负荷削减反而超过CH4网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究氢气与天然气网络在压缩机故障这一关键扰动下，管线存气动态响应的差异，为燃气输配系统运营商（TSO）在氢气网络设计和运行中提供参考依据。

Method: 采用三天动态仿真，构建CH4网络和与电力系统集成的H2网络；设置两种对比场景：Case 1（相同管道内径）和Case 2（不同管道内径），分析压缩机故障后的瞬态恢复、管线存气、压降及负荷削减情况。

Result: 1) 两种场景下H2的瞬态恢复均快于CH4，但管线存气量少于CH4；2) 相同管径时H2压降更低，需求削减更少；3) 减小H2管径后，H2压降增大，负荷削减超过CH4网络。

Conclusion: H2网络在压缩机故障后具有更快的动态恢复能力，但管线存气容量较小；管道直径选择对H2网络的压降和供应可靠性影响显著，TSO可据此优化管网设计与运行策略。

Abstract: Linepack is a critical buffer in gas networks, providing short-term storage and operational flexibility. This paper presents a comparative dynamic analysis of the linepack impact in hydrogen (H2) and natural gas (CH4) networks under compressor contingency conditions. A three-day dynamic simulation is conducted for a CH4 network and a H2 network integrated with a power system. Two cases are investigated: Case 1 with identical and Case 2 with different pipe inner diameters in CH4 and H2 networks. The results in both cases show that H2 exhibits faster transient recovery after the compressor contingency, although its linepack is less than that of CH4. On the other hand, depending on the selected pipe diameter, pressure losses in the two networks can differ significantly. With identical pipe diameters, H2 exhibits lower pressure drop than CH4, and its demand therefore experiences less curtailment. However, when the H2 pipe diameter is reduced, the pressure drop across the H2 pipes increases and the curtailed load becomes higher than in the CH4 network. These results can be used by gas TSOs in designing and operating their grid more efficiently.

</details>


### [121] [Dynamic Speed Limit Control of Connected Automated Vehicles in Freeway Networks Considering Traffic Composition Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16615)
*Lei Wei,Yu Han,Haiyang Yu,Yunpeng Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种考虑交通组成不确定性（人工驾驶与网联自动驾驶车辆混合比例观测误差）的模型预测控制框架，用于高速公路动态速度限制控制，在单瓶颈和多瓶颈网络中均能减少行程时间并带来环境效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态速度控制方法大多假设CAV渗透率是确定且精确已知的，但实际中渗透率存在观测误差，导致混合交通组成不确定性，进而降低控制性能。亟需一种能够显式处理这种不确定性的控制方法。

Method: 首先构建不确定性感知的宏观混合交通流模型，通过混合基本图将不确定的渗透率传播至混合自由流速度、通行能力和容量下降条件，从而影响交通流动力学；然后提出考虑交通组成不确定性的模型预测控制框架，针对多个可接受的渗透率实现值优化CAV速度限制，提高控制鲁棒性。

Result: 在单瓶颈高速公路走廊和多瓶颈含合流-分流交互的高速公路网络上的仿真实验表明，所提控制器能生成空间上更协调的速度限制，有效减少总行程时间并提供环境效益。

Conclusion: 显式考虑交通组成不确定性能够提升动态速度限制控制在混合交通环境中的鲁棒性和有效性，为实际部署提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Dynamic speed limit control has emerged as a promising strategy to improve freeway sustainability in mixed traffic environments with connected automated vehicles (CAVs). However, most existing approaches assume that the CAV penetration rate is deterministic and can be accurately known throughout the control horizon. In reality, the penetration rate has inherent observation errors, leading to uncertainty in mixed traffic composition, which in turn degrades control performance. To overcome this limitation, this study proposes a novel model predictive control (MPC) framework for dynamic CAV speed limit control in freeway networks that explicitly incorporates traffic composition uncertainty into both flow prediction and control optimization. An uncertainty-aware macroscopic mixed traffic model is first developed, where the uncertain penetration rate propagates through the mixed fundamental diagram to the flow dynamics by affecting the mixed free-flow speed, capacity, and capacity drop condition. Then, a traffic composition-aware MPC is formulated to optimize CAV speed limits against multiple admissible penetration rate realizations, thereby improving control robustness under heterogeneous traffic conditions. Simulation experiments are conducted on both a single-bottleneck freeway corridor and a multi-bottleneck freeway network with merge-diverge interactions. The results demonstrate that the proposed controller generates more spatially coordinated speed limits, which effectively reduce travel time spent and provide environmental benefits.

</details>


### [122] [Cooperative MARL-Based Energy-Efficient Power Control for Two-Hop Relaying Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16703)
*Xinyu Qu,Yuanzhe Geng,Erwu Liu,Rui Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对协作通信网络中仅能获取过时部分信道状态信息的非理想场景，提出一种基于延迟奖励的多智能体深度Q网络学习框架，实现分布式中继自主决策下的能量效率最大化合作博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合作博弈方法依赖瞬时信道状态信息，但在实际通信网络中瞬时CSI难以获取，仅能获得部分可观测的过时CSI，需要在此非理想条件下实现能量效率最大化。

Method: 定义了基于延迟奖励的状态-动作值函数，构建多智能体深度Q网络学习框架，通过理论推导证明瞬时CSI下的博弈效用是所提方法的上界。

Result: 所提方法显著优于现有替代方案，与最优解的差距仅为约5.2%。

Conclusion: 所提出的延迟奖励多智能体深度强化学习框架能有效解决非理想CSI条件下的协作通信博弈问题，接近最优性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a cooperative game in the cooperative communication network, where each relay makes decisions autonomously and aims to achieve the same optimization objective of maximizing energy efficiency. We consider the non-ideal situation where instantaneous channel state information (CSI) is difficult to obtain and only partially observable outdated CSI is available. To solve this game problem, we define a delayed reward-based state-action value function and propose a multi-agent deep Q network learning framework. Then, we prove analytically that utilities obtained by game-theoretic approaches with the instantaneous CSI serve as upper bounds for those of the proposed method. Simulation results reveal that our approach considerably outperforms its potential alternatives and is only about 5.2% away from the optimal solution.

</details>


### [123] [Driver Behavior Under Traffic Complexity: Variance Decomposition and Cross-Driver Generalization for Driver Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16847)
*Lukas Köning,Nataša Miličić,Klaus Bogenberger*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过分析175个驾驶员行为特征，发现交通复杂度仅解释行为变异的1.5%，而驾驶员个体差异占23%，剩余75%为残差方差。最终只有引导注视率一个特征具备部署可行性，所有个性化策略和分类模型在跨被试验证中均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通复杂度指标仅关注外部环境因素，而驾驶员监控系统只检测内源性状态（如分心），两者都无法解释外部需求如何转化为驾驶员感知的情境负荷。驾驶员行为是连接两者的天然桥梁，因此本文试图从行为信号反向推断交通复杂度。

Method: 对20名驾驶员在真实城市交通中的数据，系统筛选了5个域（注视、头部姿态、纵向控制、引导注视、扫描策略）共175个行为特征。使用等效检验确认零效应，通过混合效应方差分解分析变异来源，并评估8种特征级个性化策略和4种分类架构的性能。

Result: 31个特征表现出统计上显著的复杂度效应，140个被确认为零效应。复杂度仅解释1.5%行为变异，驾驶员身份占23%，残差占75%。所有个性化策略均失败，分类架构在留一被试交叉验证下F1分数约0.45。引导注视率是唯一具备部署可行性的特征。

Conclusion: 方差结构（个体差异主导、复杂度效应微弱）是复杂度估计的主要瓶颈。论文定义了复杂度自适应高级驾驶辅助系统的三种部署机制，并指出引导注视率因速度鲁棒性、跨驾驶员普适性和个体间复杂度敏感性差异小而成为最佳候选特征。

Abstract: Understanding why a traffic situation is demanding for the human driver is central to safe and comfortable partially automated driving. Existing complexity metrics characterize only external environmental factors, while driver monitoring systems detect only endogenous states such as distraction. Neither side captures how external demands translate into driver-perceived situational load. Driver behavior serves as the natural bridge between both, and this paper investigates the inverse inference problem of estimating traffic complexity from behavioral signals. A systematic screening of 175 behavioral features across five domains (gaze, head pose, longitudinal control, guiding fixation, scanning strategy) is applied to data from 20 drivers in real urban traffic. Of these, 31 features exhibit statistically confirmed complexity effects, while 140 are confirmed as null effects through equivalence testing. Mixed-effects variance decomposition reveals that complexity explains only 1.5% of behavioral variance, whereas driver identity accounts for 23% and residual variance for 75%. This unfavorable ratio explains both the failure of all eight evaluated feature-level personalization strategies and the convergence of four classification architectures at F1 around 0.45 under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. Guiding fixation rate emerges as the single most deployment-ready feature, combining speed-robustness, universality across drivers, and minimal inter-driver variation in complexity sensitivity. The results define three deployment regimes for complexity-adaptive advanced driver assistance systems and establish the variance structure as the primary bottleneck for complexity estimation.

</details>


### [124] [Learning to Stay Fresh: A Self-Learning Semantic Framework for Underwater Internet of Things](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16902)
*Ananya Hazarika,Mehdi Rahmati*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语义贝叶斯优化（SBO）与多传输贝叶斯优化（MTBO）的三层自学习语义框架，利用信息年龄（AoI）作为新鲜度度量，结合混合深度神经网络-高斯过程代理模型和传播感知AoI度量，解决水下物联网（UIoT）中异构节点与声学通信带来的互操作性挑战，实现高效实时环境监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非传统物联网（NC IoT）范式强调信息有用性而非高数据量采集，但水下物联网（UIoT）面临缺乏标准化协议、水下节点异构性强以及声学通信固有的传播延迟和带宽限制等挑战，导致互操作性问题突出，亟需新的解决方案。

Method: 提出三层自学习语义框架：1）采用语义贝叶斯优化（SBO）与多传输贝叶斯优化（MTBO）相结合的方法；2）以信息年龄（AoI）作为信息新鲜度度量指标；3）集成混合深度神经网络-高斯过程（DNN-GP）代理模型；4）制定传播感知AoI度量以增强实时环境监测性能。

Result: 通过与其他竞争方法进行对比，量化证明了所提方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自学习语义框架能够有效解决UIoT网络中的互操作性问题，在实时环境监测中优于现有方法，为非传统IoT范式在水下环境的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The emerging paradigm of Non-Conventional Internet of Things (NC IoT), which focuses on the usefulness of information rather than high-volume data collection and transmission, will be a dominant paradigm in the next generation of wireless systems. On the downside, the absence of standardized protocols and the heterogeneity of underwater nodes, coupled with the unique constraints of acoustic communication (e.g., propagation delays and bandwidth limitations), pose significant interoperability challenges for Underwater Internet of Things (UIoT) networks. In this paper, we introduce a pioneering self-learning semantic framework that addresses these issues by leveraging a three-layer architecture using a novel Semantic Bayesian Optimization (SBO) approach integrated with Multi-Transmission Bayesian Optimization (MTBO). This framework employs Age of Information (AoI) as a freshness metric, incorporates a hybrid deep neural network-Gaussian process surrogate model, and formulates a propagation-aware Aol metric to enhance real-time environmental monitoring. Results are provided and compared with other competing methods to quantify the proposed method's superiority.

</details>


### [125] [A conditional no-go for resource-free magic-axis measurement on a static surface code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16968)
*Jiachen Shen,Hui Zhong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个条件性的不可行定理：在没有折叠或自对偶结构的静态表面码补丁上，无法执行魔法态培育所需的魔法轴检查，同时还能保持高接受率。该结论在单次稳定子测量记录下被严格证明，在自适应、后选择的有限深度模型中则在两个结构假设和一个次临界假设下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证一个民间传说（folklore）——即魔法态培育中测量魔法轴的操作必须通过折叠或自对偶补丁来实现。由于容错量子计算的大部分成本用于制造魔法态，而培育技术（cultivation）通过原位测量魔法轴来降低成本，因此弄清这一必要条件是否真实存在对量子计算架构设计至关重要。

Method: 通过拓扑解读接受结果（accepted outcome）来证明不可行定理。首先对单次稳定子测量记录进行直接证明；然后扩展到自适应、后选择协议的有界深度（多项式时空体积）模型，在引入两个结构假设和一个次临界假设的条件下完成证明。最后隔离出一个尚存的公开假设，并论证保护（protection）本身不足以迫使该假设成立。

Result: 证明了一个条件性的不可行定理：无折叠或自对偶结构的静态表面码补丁无法同时实现魔法轴检查和高接受率。有用检查必须为魔法轴"付出代价"：要么添加电荷转换资源（charge-converting resource），要么离开其历史可接受的稀释区域（dilute regime），要么只能接受指数级稀有的事件。

Conclusion: 民间传说被验证为基本正确——魔法轴检查确实需要特殊结构支撑。研究将尚存的不确定性浓缩为一个孤立的猜想（conjecture），明确了未来需要解决的关键阈值问题。

Abstract: Under stated assumptions, a static surface-code patch that adds no fold or \mbox{self-dual} structure cannot perform the magic-axis check that magic-state cultivation relies on while still accepting often. This is a conditional no-go. Fault-tolerant machines spend much of their cost making magic states, and cultivation makes them in place by measuring the magic axis, which every known construction does through a fold or \mbox{self-dual} patch that it is folklore to call necessary. We test the folklore. The no-go says that a useful check must pay for the magic axis somewhere. It can add a charge-converting resource, it can leave the dilute regime of its accepted history, or it can accept only exponentially rarely. For a single stabilizer-measurement transcript this is proved outright, from a topological reading of the accepted outcome. For adaptive, post-selected protocols in a bounded-depth (polynomial spacetime-volume) model, it holds under two structural assumptions plus a subcriticality assumption. We isolate the one open assumption, show that protection alone does not force it, and give the threshold any resolution must address. What remains is a single conjecture.

</details>


### [126] [Cluster-Based Distributed Small-Signal Stability Certificates for Grid-Forming Inverter Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16985)
*Bhathiya Rathnayake,Sijia Geng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向构网型逆变器网络的时域小信号稳定认证框架，允许按集群分辨率选择性认证稳定性。通过将网络分解为电压子系统和角频率子系统，分别利用循环小增益定理和能量论证，实现了从分散式到集中式的统一稳定性判据，无需全局特征值计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电力网络常按地域、所有权或控制权进行组织，获取完整全局模型进行稳定性认证十分困难。现有方法依赖全局特征值计算，难以适应这种分布式运维结构。

Method: 在相位一致的同步工作点线性化后，利用小角度近似将模型解耦为电压子系统和角频率子系统。角频率子系统通过对称加权拉普拉斯网络结构的能量论证认证；电压子系统引入节点到节点增益，利用循环小增益论证推导出从完全分散式到集群式再到集中式的一系列充分指数稳定判据。对于任意网络分区，每个集群验证集群内有向环条件和集群间路径条件。

Result: 单节点和单集群极限分别恢复分散式和集中式证书。稳定性指标不仅提供通过/未通过判定，还能将限制性裕度定位到单个节点、内部反馈回路和集群间通道，提供诊断信息。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在无需全局特征值计算的前提下，以与网络组织和运行相同的尺度认证稳定性，各集群仅需利用集群内信息和有限的边界信息即可完成验证，最终汇聚为网络级稳定证书。

Abstract: Large-scale power networks are often organized by geography, ownership, or control authority, making stability certificates that require a fully assembled global model challenging. This paper develops a time-domain small-signal stability certification framework for grid-forming inverter networks with selectable clustering resolution. The objective is to certify stability at the same scale at which the network is organized and operated: each cluster verifies conditions using intra-cluster and limited boundary information, and these checks collectively yield a network-level stability certificate without requiring a global eigenvalue computation. After linearization about a phase-cohesive synchronized operating point, a small-angle approximation decomposes the model into a voltage subsystem and an angle-frequency subsystem, where the latter is certified by an energy argument using the symmetric weighted-Laplacian network structure. For the voltage subsystem, node-to-node gains are introduced and a cyclic small-gain argument yields a family of sufficient exponential stability certificates ranging from fully decentralized to cluster-based and centralized. For an arbitrary network partition, each cluster verifies intra-cluster directed-cycle conditions and inter-cluster path conditions. The singleton- and single-cluster limits recover the decentralized and centralized certificates, respectively. The resulting stability indices provide diagnostic information beyond a pass/fail verdict by localizing the limiting margin to individual nodes, internal feedback loops, and inter-cluster channels.

</details>


### [127] [Increasing Line Outage Localization Performance with Ensemble Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17008)
*Daniel Flores,Yuanrui Sang,Michael P. McGarry*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用集成分类器提升输电线路故障定位性能的方法，通过三种算法（贪婪MCP、high-eta、随机）选择观测线路，并基于LODF和LOIF两种灵敏度因子进行实验。结果表明，贪婪MCP算法选择的观测线路F1分数最高，集成分类器显著优于单一kNN模型，其中Extra-Trees Bagging在多数情况下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际电力系统中，一条输电线路的故障可通过监测另一条线路的潮流来定位，但存在不确定性。传统单模型方法在区分不同故障情况时性能有限，因此需要探索集成学习方法以提升定位准确性。

Method: 采用三种观测线路选择算法（贪婪MCP、high-eta、随机）结合两种灵敏度因子（LODF、LOIF），对比多种集成分类器与基准kNN分类器在输电线路故障定位中的性能，使用F1分数作为评价指标。

Result: 贪婪MCP算法选择的观测线路获得的F1分数最高；集成分类器整体显著优于单一kNN模型；Extra-Trees Bagging技术在多数实例中取得最高F1分数；所有结果均具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 集成分类器能有效提升输电线路故障定位性能，贪婪MCP算法是最优的观测线路选择策略，Extra-Trees Bagging是推荐的高性能集成方法。

Abstract: In many cases, the outage of one transmission line in a system can be localized by monitoring the power flow of another line, and machine learning methods can be used to distinguish the cases under uncertainty. In this study, we examine the improvements in line outage localization performance achieved by various ensemble classifiers compared to single-model methods. In the case studies, we compared the classification results with measurement data collected at observed transmission lines (OTLs) selected using three algorithms, i.e, greedy maximum coverage problem (MCP), high-eta, and random selection, based on two sensitivity factors, i.e., line outage distribution factors (LODFs) and line outage impact factors (LOIFs). We found that the OTLs selected by the greedy MCP algorithm yielded the highest F1 score and the ensemble classifiers significantly outperformed a base kNN classifier. The extra-trees bagging technique achieved the highest F1 score in many instances. All the findings were statistically significant.

</details>


### [128] [Feasibility-Aware Security-Constrained Unit Commitment via Hybrid Soft Actor-Critic with Quantum-Sampled Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17031)
*George Dimas,Amin Masoumi,Mert Korkali*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三层混合框架，将伯努利混合软演员-评论家（HSAC）策略用于机组启停决策、量子采样辅助通道增强状态、原生SCUC混合整数线性规划恢复调度与安全变量。该方法在有限强制子集下求解，是求解器兼容而非端到端替代方案。在14、57、118节点系统中评估，发现探索性伯努利演员和小强制上限下的有用启停信息量是制约可扩展性的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全约束机组组合（SCUC）问题耦合二进制启停决策、经济调度、备用和网络安全，精确求解在大规模实际系统中计算代价过高。

Method: 提出三层混合框架：第一层使用伯努利混合软演员-评论家（HSAC）策略提出每小时的启停决策；第二层用量子采样辅助通道增强状态表示；第三层在仅强制少量启停二进制变量后，用原生SCUC混合整数线性规划恢复调度和网络安全变量。

Result: 14节点系统恢复的调度方案达到全时域最优；57节点系统屏幕拒绝率极低；118节点系统在强制上限无法覆盖完整启停周期时出现明显的覆盖瓶颈。

Conclusion: 探索性伯努利演员和小强制上限下，传递给恢复模型的有用启停信息量是制约可扩展性的主导限制因素。

Abstract: Security-constrained unit commitment (SCUC) couples binary commitment, economic dispatch, reserves, and network security over a multiperiod horizon, making an exact solution computationally expensive for realistic system sizes. This paper proposes a three-layer hybrid framework in which a Bernoulli hybrid soft actor-critic (HSAC) policy proposes hourly commitments, a quantum-sampled auxiliary channel augments the state, and a native SCUC mixed-integer linear program recovers dispatch and security variables after only a limited subset of commitment binaries is enforced. The method is therefore solver-compatible rather than an end-to-end replacement for exact optimization. We formalize the SCUC-to-reinforcement-learning interface, derive the temporal coverage induced by the fixed cap, and evaluate the 14- 57- and 118-bus benchmark cases. The results show stable, low-cost recovery in the 14-bus case, where the best recovered schedule attains the full-horizon optimum; a very low screen-rejection rate in the 57-bus case; and a clear coverage bottleneck in the 118-bus case once the enforcement cap no longer spans a complete commitment period. The study, therefore, identifies the amount of useful commitment information that reaches the recovery model, under an exploratory Bernoulli actor and a small enforcement cap, as the dominant limitation that governs scalability

</details>


### [129] [Optimal Safety Control using High-Order Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17032)
*Neng Li,Zuodong Pan,Jiaxing Wang,Weiguo Xia,Wei Ren*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性控制系统的最优安全控制问题，提出了新型高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBFs），并利用向量Lyapunov函数方法设计了高阶控制Lyapunov函数（HOCLF），通过兼容性分析实现安全与稳定的协同最优控制，并用四旋翼导航算例验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零化型高阶控制屏障函数存在局限性，需要探索更高效的新型高阶控制屏障函数，并解决安全控制与稳定控制之间的兼容性问题，以实现非线性控制系统的最优安全控制。

Method: 提出两种新型高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBFs），以显式方式设计安全控制器；采用向量Lyapunov函数方法构建高阶控制Lyapunov函数（HOCLF）；讨论所提HOCBF与现有方法的关联；通过兼容性分析实现HOCLF与HOCBF的协调，建立最优控制器。

Result: 通过四旋翼导航问题的数值算例，验证了所提方法在同时保证系统稳定性和安全性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的新型高阶控制屏障函数和控制Lyapunov函数能够有效解决非线性控制系统的安全与稳定协同最优控制问题，具有良好的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: This paper investigates the optimal safety control problem of nonlinear control systems by proposing novel high-order control barrier functions (HOCBFs). Different from zeroing HOCBFs, two novel HOCBFs are derived and the safety controllers are designed in an explicit way. Next, we implement vector Lyapunov function approach to propose a novel high-order control Lyapunov function (HOCLF) for the stabilization control problem. The relations between the proposed and existing HOCBFs are discussed. Afterwards, the compatibility of the proposed HOCLF and HOCBF is addressed to guarantee the stabilization and safety control objectives simultaneously, and thus the optimal controller is established. Finally, a numerical example from the navigation problem of quadrotors is presented to illustrate the efficacy of the derived results.

</details>


### [130] [A recursive subspace based method for errors-in-variables model identification of time-varying systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17065)
*Deepanjhan Das,Shankar Narasimhan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种递归子空间模型辨识算法（RSMI-IPCA），能够在误差变量（EIV）设定下在线估计多输入多输出（MIMO）线性状态空间模型及其时变噪声方差、模型阶次和参数，适用于监控缓慢或突变的时变系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SMI-IPCA算法在误差变量设定下能同时辨识线性状态空间模型和异方差噪声方差，但仅限于批处理（离线）方式，无法适应在线场景和时变系统。本文旨在将其拓展为递归形式，实现实时在线辨识和自适应模型更新。

Method: 提出RSMI-IPCA算法：采用固定长度的滑动窗口（而非存储全部历史数据），递归更新主成分分析结果，在每一步新数据到达时同时估计测量噪声方差、过程阶次和状态空间矩阵。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该算法能够有效适应传感器缓慢退化（时变噪声方差）、过程操作条件变化（时变模型参数）以及结构修改（变动的模型阶次），验证了其实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: RSMI-IPCA实现了对时变系统的在线自适应辨识，兼具对缓慢和突然变化的鲁棒跟踪能力，为实际工业过程的实时监控与模型更新提供了一种有效工具。

Abstract: The Subspace-based Model Identification algorithm using a modified Iterative Principal Component Analysis (SMI-IPCA) is a theoretically rigorous method for identifying a linear state-space model of a multi-input multi-output (MIMO) process, in an errors-in-variables (EIV) setting. The method can simultaneously estimate unknown heteroskedastic noise variances corrupting the input and output measurements, along with the state space model. This work proposes a recursive formulation of SMI-IPCA (RSMI-IPCA) enabling online identification and adaptive model updates as and when new data arrive. By maintaining a fixed length lag window rather than storing the complete historical data, RSMI-IPCA estimates measurement noise variances, process order, while simultaneously identifying the state-space matrices, making it suitable to monitor time-varying systems, whether the induced changes are slow or abrupt. The algorithm gradually adapts to slow sensor degradation (time-varying noise variances), changes in process operating conditions (time-varying model parameters), and structural modifications (varying model order). Simulation studies are presented to demonstrate the efficacy and practical applicability of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [131] [Deep Reinforcement Learning-Based Energy Management for Hydrogen-Enabled Community Microgrids Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17248)
*Mohamed Atef,Sanath Alahakoon,Umme Mumtahina,Peter Wolfs,Tamer Khatib,Moslem Uddin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于近端策略优化（PPO）的社区微电网能量管理系统，用于协调电池、氢能和柴油发电，在澳大利亚Rockhampton的1000户社区场景下实现了净年运营收入A$195,690.67、可再生能源占比91.2%、碳强度0.085 kg CO2/kWh及99.77%的负荷满足率；在电网故障概率升至5%时仍能维持98.79%的供电，但展示了策略对可再生能源波动性、训练稳定性和评估边界的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢能社区微电网能够提升可再生能源利用率和本地韧性，但其运行面临间歇性发电、不确定的居民需求、动态电价以及电池与氢能储能的耦合动力学等复杂挑战，亟需开发有效的协调调度策略。

Method: 将能量管理问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），包含11维状态空间和三个连续控制动作（电池、柴油、氢气调度），电网交换由剩余功率平衡确定；采用近端策略优化（PPO）算法进行策略学习，并使用澳大利亚Rockhampton地区1000户社区8,760小时的真实数据评估。

Result: 正常场景下净年运营收入A$195,690.67，可再生能源占比91.2%，碳强度0.085 kg CO2/kWh，负荷满足率99.77%。电网故障概率从1%升至5%时，净运营收入保持A$169,892.21，供电率98.79%，电池放电量增加413%，柴油发电量增加429%。

Conclusion: PPO能够有效协调电池与氢能的联合调度，在正常和故障场景下均表现出较好的经济性和韧性；但策略性能对可再生能源波动性、训练稳定性及评估边界选取较为敏感。

Abstract: Hydrogen-enabled community microgrids can improve renewable energy utilization and local resilience, but their operation is complicated by intermittent generation, uncertain residential demand, dynamic electricity prices, and the coupled dynamics of battery and hydrogen storage. This paper develops a proximal policy optimization (PPO)-based energy management system (EMS) for a grid-connected community microgrid integrating photovoltaic and wind generation, battery storage, an electrolyzer, hydrogen storage, a fuel cell, and diesel backup. The EMS is formulated as a Markov decision process with an 11-dimensional state and three continuous control actions for battery, diesel, and hydrogen dispatch; grid exchange is determined from the residual power balance. The framework is evaluated using 8,760 hourly observations for a 1,000-household community in Rockhampton, Australia. Under the normal operating scenario, the learned policy produced a net annual operating revenue of A$195,690.67, a renewable fraction of 91.2%, a carbon intensity of 0.085 kg CO2/kWh under the adopted accounting boundary, and 99.77% load satisfaction. With the hourly grid-outage probability increased from 1% to 5%, the policy retained A$169,892.21 in net operating revenue and supplied 98.79% of demand, supported by a 413% increase in battery discharge and a 429% increase in diesel generation. The results demonstrate the potential of PPO for coordinated battery-hydrogen dispatch while also highlighting sensitivity to renewable-profile variability, training stability, and the choice of evaluation boundary.

</details>


### [132] [A Phased Development Framework Enabling Islanded Operation of Sustainable AI Data Centers With Onsite Grid-Following and Grid-Forming Energy Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17391)
*Soham Ghosh,Nabil Mohammed,Mohammad Ashraf Hossain Sadi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向超大规模AI数据中心的分阶段部署框架，通过模块化建设、混合现场发电（天然气+构网型储能）及电磁暂态仿真验证，解决EPC过程中电网互联审批和设备采购周期长等瓶颈，支持500MW至2GW级设施在2030年前实现约50GW并网目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注AI数据中心的环境影响和电网交互潜力，但缺乏对其通过工程、采购和建设（EPC）流程实现规模化部署所面临挑战的系统性分析。

Method: 提出分阶段扩展框架，采用模块化建筑架构和混合现场发电（天然气+构网型储能）；利用电磁暂态仿真（EMT）评估系统性能。

Result: 现场天然气发电与构网型储能的组合可在早期和中期部署阶段可靠支持数据中心运行；具备孤岛运行能力以应对电网扰动；比较了不同构网型控制策略在系统重连与恢复中的表现。

Conclusion: 该分阶段框架能有效帮助开发者在满足紧迫上市时间目标的同时，应对多并行年的电网互联审批和设备采购周期限制。

Abstract: As hyperscale and colocation AI data centers continue to expand, the electric grid is increasingly required to support large, concentrated loads, with individual facilities ranging from 500 MW to 2 GW. Current projections estimate that approximately 50 GW of AI data center capacity will require grid connectivity in the United States by 2030. While prior research has extensively examined the environmental and operational impacts of AI data centers, as well as their potential role as grid-interactive assets, limited attention has been given to the challenges associated with their scalable deployment through engineering, procurement, and construction (EPC) processes. This manuscript addresses this gap by proposing a phased development framework for AI data center expansion. The approach is designed to enable developers to meet aggressive time-to-market objectives while navigating multi-year constraints associated with interconnection approvals and lead times associated with the procurement of component equipment. A modular construction architecture is presented, along with a detailed analysis of integrated energy systems and the role of hybrid on-site generation in supporting incremental capacity growth. Electromagnetic transient simulations (EMT) are used to evaluate system performance, demonstrating that a combination of on-site natural gas generation and grid-forming energy storage can reliably support data center operations during early and intermediate deployment phases. The study further examines the transition to full grid interconnection, including the capability of the data center to operate in islanded mode during grid disturbances. Finally, the manuscript compares grid-forming control strategies for system reconnection and restoration under varying conditions.

</details>


### [133] [An Update to the Level Set Theorems in Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17435)
*Dylan Hirsch,William McEneaney,Jaime Fisac,Claire Tomlin,Sylvia Herbert*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文讨论了Hamilton-Jacobi可达性（HJR）框架在安全关键系统控制中的应用，并指出其Level Set定理成立需要满足额外的技术标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保HJR框架中Level Set定理的理论严谨性，指出其成立所需的额外条件。

Method: 技术性更新，补充HJR中Level Set定理成立所需的额外标准。

Result: 明确了Level Set定理成立需要满足的额外准则。

Conclusion: HJR框架的Level Set定理在实际应用中需要满足额外的技术条件才能成立。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability (HJR) is an important framework for controlling safety-critical systems despite uncertainty. Its theoretical underpinnings are rooted in Hamilton-Jacobi Partial Differential Equations, which provide the value function used for controller synthesis. The Level Set Theorems of HJR allow one to interpret the value function in terms of satisfaction of a qualitative goal (e.g. goal-reaching or obstacle-avoidance). We here provide a technical update regarding additional criteria needed for these theorems to hold.

</details>


### [134] [Multi-scale closed-loop melt pool control for LPBF via policy optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17438)
*Junan Lin,Riccardo Zuliani,Baris Kavas,Markus Bambach,John Lygeros,Efe C. Balta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于激光粉末床熔融（LPBF）工艺的双环数据驱动控制策略，结合层内线性输出反馈控制（策略梯度优化增益）与层间前馈控制（温度轨迹优化+迭代学习控制），在仿真和实验中均实现了表面温度的稳定控制，相比贝叶斯优化基线将平均跟踪误差降低3.4%、输入约束违规减少47.5%，并且是首次将sim-to-real策略优化成功应用于LPBF工艺的研究之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPBF工艺中温度稳定对避免变形和开裂等缺陷至关重要，但现有控制方法依赖手动调参，在打印复杂几何形状时易导致零件失效，亟需一种鲁棒且近最优的自适应控制策略。

Method: 提出双环数据驱动控制策略：(i) 层内线性输出反馈控制，通过策略梯度优化增益；(ii) 层间前馈控制，结合温度轨迹优化与迭代学习控制。控制器完全离线调优，仅使用单个校准层的未受控打印数据。

Result: 仿真显示该方法在模型失配和测量噪声下仍能有效稳定温度；实验表明，在硬件受限的简化版本下，相比贝叶斯优化基线，平均跟踪误差降低3.4%，平均输入约束违规减少47.5%。此外发现了高频激励动力学可减少矢量头膨胀的新现象。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着sim-to-real策略优化在LPBF工艺中的首批成功应用之一，为增材制造社区开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Laser powder bed fusion (LPBF) is a metal additive manufacturing process where temperature stabilization is of vital importance to avoid defects such as distortion and cracking. Existing control methods require manual tuning, increasing the risk of part failure when printing complex geometries. This paper introduces a dual-loop, data-driven control strategy to stabilize the surface temperature, ensuring robustness and near-optimal performance in the presence of disturbances. The proposed method integrates (i) an in-layer linear output feedback control with gains optimized through policy gradient, and (ii) a layer-to-layer feedforward control combining temperature trajectory optimization and iterative learning control. Simulation results show that the multi-scale controller effectively stabilizes the temperature even under significant model mismatch and measurement noise. Experimental results demonstrate that a simplified, hardware-constrained version of this method matches the state-of-the-art performance of in-situ data-driven methods, reducing mean tracking error by 3.4% and mean input-constraint violation by 47.5% relative to a Bayesian Optimization-tuned baseline. For this physical LPBF validation, the controller is tuned entirely offline using uncontrolled print data from a single calibration layer. Our experiments also demonstrate a new class of high-frequency excitation dynamics that result in reduced vector head swelling, opening up new avenues of research in the additive manufacturing community. This work marks one of the first successful applications of sim-to-real policy optimization in LPBF processes.

</details>


### [135] [Topology-Aware Propagation-Based Assessment of Extreme-Weather Impacts on Distribution System Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17565)
*Junjie Yin,Xinyu Feng,Jian Han,Fangxing Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对暴雨事件下配电系统的、集成概率事件追踪建模、支路级故障筛选、下游影响传播分析和运行影响评估的统一框架，能够在不确定性下区分地理暴露与拓扑依赖的运行影响，并支持渐进式预警评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气事件和分布式能源的广泛接入给配电系统带来了日益增长的不确定性和韧性挑战。现有方法多聚焦于确定性故障分析，缺乏在预报不确定性下考虑拓扑感知的运行影响评估。

Method: 提出事件条件不确定性建模与拓扑感知影响传播框架，包含概率事件追踪建模、支路级故障筛选、下游影响传播分析以及运行影响评估四个核心模块，并在IEEE 33节点馈线上进行案例验证。

Result: 该框架能够区分地理暴露与拓扑依赖的运行影响，支持不确定场景下的渐进式早期预警影响评估，并在CURENT LTB-AGVis平台上可视化影响区域。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了天气驱动事件中地理暴露与拓扑传播影响的解耦问题，为配电系统在极端天气下的韧性评估和预警提供了新的方法论支持。

Abstract: Extreme weather events and the increasing integration of distributed energy resources (DERs) introduce growing uncertainty and resilience challenges for distribution systems. Unlike conventional deterministic contingencies, weather-driven disruptions exhibit probabilistic and spatial-temporal characteristics, where outage consequences depend on both geographic exposure and feeder topology. Existing approaches commonly focus on deterministic outage analysis, while topology-aware operational impact assessment under forecast uncertainty remains limited. This paper proposes an event-conditioned uncertainty modeling and topology-aware impact propagation framework for distribution systems under torrential rain events. The proposed framework integrates probabilistic event-track modeling, branch-level fault screening, downstream impact propagation analysis, and operational impact assessment within a unified workflow. Finally, case studies on the IEEE 33-bus distribution feeder demonstrate that the proposed framework can distinguish geographic exposure from topology-dependent operational impacts and support progressive early-warning impact assessment under uncertain scenarios. Furthermore, the impact zones are visualized on the CURENT Large-scale Testbed (LTB)-AGVis platform.

</details>


### [136] [Online learning of neural state-space models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17614)
*Bendegúz Györök,Tamás Péni,Maarten Schoukens,Roland Tóth*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子空间编码器的ANN-SS模型批量学习流程与直接递推辨识算法，实现了高效在线自适应，并通过收敛性分析与仿真验证了其高精度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度学习的非线性系统辨识方法（如ANN-SS模型）在离线设置中表现优异，但在线学习方面尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出了一种批量学习流程和直接递推辨识算法，用于子空间编码器-based ANN-SS模型，并对其递推形式进行了收敛性分析。

Result: 所提出的方法在计算效率高的在线自适应中实现了高模型精度，仿真研究验证了其性能。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了ANN-SS模型在线学习的空白，为非线性系统在线辨识提供了有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep-learning-based nonlinear system identification have led to encoder-based estimation of neural state-space (ANN-SS) models that achieve state-of-the-art performance in offline settings by estimating initial model states from past input-output data. These methods are typically used in multiple-shooting-based offline identification, and online learning of these models remains largely unexplored. This paper presents a batch-wise learning pipeline and a direct recursive identification algorithm for subspace encoder-based ANN-SS models. We provide convergence analysis of the recursive formulation and validate its performance through extensive simulation studies. The results demonstrate that the proposed approach enables computationally efficient online adaptation with high model accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [On Optimal Event-Triggered Distributed Control for Stochastic Multi-Agent Systems via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17635)
*Ziming Wang,Bingbing Li,Karl H. Johansson,Apostolos I. Rikos*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的多智能体系统最优分布式控制算法，采用演员-评论家-辨识器结构处理随机不确定性，并引入低通滤波器和混合事件触发控制策略，通过李雅普诺夫稳定性证明确保跟踪误差有界，在单轴机器人仿真中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多智能体系统随机不确定性和非仿射非线性故障时存在局限性，且分布式控制策略的通信资源消耗较高，亟需一种兼具最优性、鲁棒性和资源高效性的控制算法。

Method: 采用演员-评论家-辨识器（actor-critic-identifier）结构：演员网络反映控制行为，评论家网络评估控制性能，辨识器网络处理随机不确定性；引入低通滤波器抑制非仿射非线性故障的影响；提出混合事件触发控制策略降低控制频率；基于李雅普诺夫方法进行稳定性证明。

Result: 通过李雅普诺夫稳定性分析证明了所有误差有界，实现了领导者与跟随者之间的精确跟踪；在单轴机器人操纵器仿真中验证了算法的正确性，并与非最优控制算法对比，显示了所提最优控制算法的运行优势。

Conclusion: 该算法有效解决了带随机不确定性的多智能体系统最优分布式控制问题，在保证跟踪精度的同时降低了通信与控制频率，具有理论完备性和实际应用价值。
